The study identifies limits on the incremental value of these trajectory summaries and separates allocation quality from measured inference benefit.

</details>


### [69] [Twist Flow for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09281)
*Shiqin Zeng,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 joint twist-flow，一种增广流匹配方法，通过从 (z_x, y) 到 (x, z_y) 的连续传输建模后验采样，缓解条件生成模型后验变异度不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯反问题中，后验采样既要与观测一致，又要覆盖合理的解空间。直接条件生成模型虽引入隐噪声建模歧义，但成对训练易退化为近确定性映射，导致样本虽与观测一致却低估后验变异度，在多峰后验下出现欠覆盖、模态扭曲或伪过渡。

Method: 提出 joint twist-flow：增广流匹配形式，学习从增广源状态 (z_x, y) 到增广终态 (x, z_y) 的连续传输。x 为目标变量，y 为观测，z_x 为后验采样的高斯参考坐标，z_y 为与观测分支关联的高斯似然侧坐标；在高斯观测模型下 z_y 由归一化观测残差导出，用于将生成样本与观测一致性耦合，在不替代 x 不确定性的前提下减少似然不一致的变异并保留弱约束方向的可变性。

Result: 在带参考后验样本的低维反问题上，joint twist-flow 比直接条件流基线更好地保持多峰后验支撑；在图像恢复与地震地下速度模型反演上，在保持观测一致性的同时提升了后验变异度。

Conclusion: 通过增广状态上的联合传输（耦合观测侧坐标 z_y），joint twist-flow 能同时兼顾观测一致性与后验变异度，在低维与高维反演任务上均优于直接条件流基线。

Abstract: In Bayesian inverse problems, posterior sampling requires generating samples that are consistent with given observations while capturing the range of plausible solutions. Direct conditional generative models introduce latent noise to model this ambiguity, but paired inverse-problem training can still encourage an almost deterministic map from the observation to the target. As a result, generated samples may be observation-consistent while under-representing posterior variability, especially when the posterior is multimodal, leading to undercoverage, mode distortion, or artificial transitions between distinct feasible solutions. We propose joint twist-flow, an augmented flow-matching formulation that learns a continuous transport from the augmented source state $(z_x, y)$ to the augmented terminal state $(x, z_y)$. Here x is the target variable, $y$ is the observation, $z_x$ is the Gaussian reference coordinate for posterior sampling, and $z_y$ is a Gaussian likelihood-side coordinate associated with the observation branch. Under a Gaussian observation model, $z_y$ is motivated by the normalized observation residual associated with observation compatibility. Its role is not to replace uncertainty in $x$, but to couple generated samples of x to observation consistency, helping reduce likelihood-inconsistent variation while preserving variability in weakly constrained directions. We validate the method on low-dimensional inverse problems with reference posterior samples, where joint twist-flow better preserves multimodal posterior support than a direct conditional-flow baseline. We further evaluate the method on image restoration and seismic subsurface velocity-model inversion, showing increased posterior variability while maintaining observation consistency.

</details>


### [70] [Self-attention summary networks for subsurface velocity-model building from common-image gathers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09282)
*Shiqin Zeng,Yunlin Zeng,Abhinav Prakash Gahlot,Zijun Deng,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D共成像点道集压缩为条件嵌入，用于概率速度反演，并以流匹配模型学习后验分布，优于直接使用原始道集的条件方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共成像点道集（CIGs）通过反射层聚焦与剩余动校正蕴含速度模型误差的物理信息，但在常规成像流程中通常仅作诊断工具，未被有效用于速度反演。同时，直接以高维原始CIG作为条件会带来背景速度不匹配等干扰，限制后验推断质量。

Method: 构建多尺度自注意力摘要网络，将高维3D CIG数据体映射为紧凑的条件嵌入，保留随偏移距变化的运动学结构与空间相干性，并抑制背景速度不匹配导致的变异性。以该嵌入为条件，使用流匹配模型学习从高斯源分布到合理速度场后验分布的传输映射。

Result: 数值实验表明，相比直接以原始CIG作为条件，所提摘要网络提升后验速度推断效果；多尺度注意力设计对背景模型不匹配具有更强鲁棒性，获得更准确的后验重建及更低的预测不确定性。

Conclusion: 将CIG从诊断工具转化为可学习条件摘要，可为概率地下速度反演提供有效信息压缩与条件化途径，多尺度自注意力在抑制背景模型误差影响方面具有优势。

Abstract: Common-image gathers (CIGs) contain physically meaningful information about velocity-model errors through reflector focusing and residual moveout, but in conventional imaging workflows they are typically used only as diagnostic tools. In this work, we propose a multiscale self-attention summary network that maps high-dimensional 3D CIG volumes into compact conditioning embeddings for probabilistic subsurface velocity inversion. These learned embeddings preserve offset-dependent kinematic structure and spatial coherence while reducing variability caused by background-velocity mismatch. Conditioned on these summary embeddings, a flow-matching model learns a transport from a Gaussian source distribution to the posterior distribution of plausible velocity fields. Numerical experiments show that, compared with direct conditioning on raw CIGs, the proposed summary network improves posterior velocity inference. In particular, the multiscale attention design provides greater robustness to background-model mismatch, yielding more accurate posterior reconstructions and lower predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [71] [LeCuration: A Tiny World Model as a Data Curation Multi-Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09285)
*Mayank Sengupta,Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出以单个数据集的世界模型作为物理AI数据治理工具的思路，构建LeCuration（基于LeWM潜变量编码器/预测器+DiT解码器），在CS:GO数据上做定性验证：嵌入可用于异常检测与内容聚类，自回归预测可用于动作-状态一致性检查；尚无定量指标与下游训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI应用多处于有限或封闭物理世界（如仓库机器人、视频游戏智能体），其数据受特定物理规律支配。为更好地组织、筛选与策展适用于此类应用的数据，需要一种围绕各数据集自身设置与物理规律的新方法。

Method: 训练小型世界模型LeCuration，作为独立下游大模型的数据策展工具：以LeWorldModel(LeWM)作潜变量编码器与预测器，并加入扩散Transformer(DiT)解码器为自回归游戏rollout生成视觉画面；利用模型嵌入做异常检测信号与基于内容的聚类启发式，并通过自回归预测游戏状态进行动作-状态一致性定性检查。

Result: 在CS:GO gameplay数据上完成定性、概念验证性案例研究：模型嵌入可作为异常检测信号与内容聚类启发式；自回归预测可定性检查动作-状态一致性。未报告定量策展指标或下游训练结果。

Conclusion: 该工作是一个定性概念验证，表明以数据集特定世界模型进行数据策展具有可行性；关键下一步是补足定量策展指标与下游训练结果。

Abstract: Many applications of physical AI run within finite or closed physical worlds with a limited set of physical laws governing object behavior. Examples include robots working in a warehouse and agents moving around in a video game. In order to better organize, filter, and curate data for physical AI applications, we propose a new approach centered on the unique settings and physical laws of individual datasets. We train LeCuration, a small world model intended to serve as a data curation tool for a separate, larger downstream model. To build this model, we choose LeWorldModel (LeWM)as our latent encoder and predictor, adding a diffusion transformer (DiT) decoder to add visuals to autoregressive gameplay rollout. We find that the embeddings of this model can be used as an anomaly detection signal and as a content-based clustering heuristic, and that auto-regressively predicting the game state with this model allows us to qualitatively check for action-state consistency. This paper presents a qualitative, proof-of-concept case study on CS:GO gameplay data; we do not yet report quantitative curation metrics or downstream training results, which we identify as the key next step.

</details>


### [72] [Node-level Graph Neural Architecture Search Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09297)
*Lintao Yanga,Sirui Lia,Yaqing Wang,Pietro Liò,Xu Shen,Baisong Liu,Chengbin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出节点级图神经架构搜索算法 N-GNAS，可为不同节点子集自动选择合适网络架构，并引入对比学习损失，在八个数据集的节点与图分类任务上优于现有 GNAS 方法及人工设计 GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GNN 与架构搜索方法对所有节点施加统一的卷积操作，忽视了节点在结构与特征上的差异性，导致模型性能下降，且随层数增加易出现过度平滑问题。

Method: 提出节点级图神经架构搜索算法 N-GNAS，在更新节点特征时为每个节点子集自动选择合适的网络架构；同时引入对比学习损失，使不同类别的样本特征相互分离、同类样本特征相互靠近。

Result: 在八个数据集的节点分类与图分类任务上进行实验，该方法性能优于当前主流 GNAS 技术及传统人工设计 GNN；例如在 CiteSeer 数据集上达到 78.26% 的准确率。

Conclusion: 节点级架构搜索能够缓解统一卷积带来的性能损失与过度平滑问题，验证了为不同节点子集自适应选择架构的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and architecture search frameworks have gained extensive application in non-Euclidean data processing, attributable to their superior capacity in managing unstructured data. Nevertheless, traditional approaches typically apply uniform convolution operations to all nodes, regardless of their varying structural and feature characteristics, which can undermine model performance and result in over-smoothing issues as the number of layers increases. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose a \textbf{N}ode-Level \textbf{G}raph \textbf{N}eural \textbf{A}rchitecture \textbf{S}earch (N-GNAS) algorithm. It can automatically choose an appropriate network architecture for each subset of nodes when updating node features. N-GNAS also introduces a contrastive learning loss to separate sample features from different categories and vice versa. In experiments conducted on eight datasets for node and graph classification, our methodology outperforms current leading GNAS techniques and traditional human-designed GNNs. For example, it achieves an accuracy rate of 78.26\% on the CiteSeer dataset.

</details>


### [73] [Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305)
*Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Mayank Sengupta,Tejal Bedmutha,Siri Reddy,Sahiti Dharmavaram,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出动作条件世界模型评测存在"Virtual2Real gap"：基于视觉相似度的指标（FVD、LPIPS、JEDi）与可玩性不相关；作者主张转向原始玩法数据的治理与诊断性属性度量，并开源了通用物理AI数据治理工具包 Kuration SDK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型基准正从视觉相似度指标转向动作语义与物理落地指标，但对域无关、任务无关的训练场景，现有基准提供的信号有限；且视觉指标无法反映真实可玩性，评测信号与训练需求脱节。

Method: 在 CounterStrike 玩法数据上训练并评测扩散世界模型，对比定性可玩性与 FVD、LPIPS、JEDi 等指标；提出不依赖可靠基准、转而在训练前对原始玩法数据进行治理并度量多类诊断属性的思路，并给出若干治理策略与开源工具包 Kuration SDK。

Result: 实验确认定性可玩性与 FVD、LPIPS、JEDi 不相对应，即 Virtual2Real gap 存在；Kuration SDK 成功定位了一个具体案例的根因——两个在相同地图、动作与状态分布上训练、LPIPS 与 FVD 分数相近的世界模型，实际游玩表现却差异显著。

Conclusion: 在缺乏可靠基准时，治理原始数据并度量诊断属性比依赖视觉相似度指标更具鲁棒性；Kuration SDK 可揭示特定数据集中 Virtual2Real gap 的根因，并加速高样本效率训练数据集的开发。

Abstract: Benchmarks for measuring the quality of action-conditioned world models are still evolving and shifting away from visual similarity-based metrics to action-semantic and physically-grounded metrics. However, for domain and task-agnostic action-conditioned world model training, existing benchmarks provide a limited signal. By training and evaluating diffusion world models on CounterStrike gameplay data, we confirm that qualitative playability does not correspond with metrics such as FVD, LPIPS, and JEDi. We term this the Virtual2Real gap. We posit that, in lieu of reliable benchmarks, curating raw gameplay data and measuring a variety of diagnostic properties provides a more robust signal to bridge the gap, before the training even begins. We present several curation strategies and a general-purpose kit for physical AI data curation called Kuration SDK, which is being open-sourced with this paper. The SDK was instrumental in uncovering the root cause of the virtual2real gap in a specific case: why two world models trained on identical gameplay map, action and state distribution, behaved very differently when played in spite of having very similar LPIPS and FVD scores. Thus, Kuration SDK has the potential to uncover the root causes of Virtual2Real gap in specific datasets and accelerate development of sample-efficient training datasets.

</details>


### [74] [MovieSTAGE: Scene, Transition, and Global Encoding for Movie-fMRI ADHD Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09306)
*Boseong Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MovieSTAGE多尺度框架，将自然电影fMRI中的场景内超图FC组织、相邻场景间无符号FC差异与全片FC结合，用于分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然电影fMRI可提供共享、时间结构化的脑动力学探针，但现有预测模型多依赖整段功能连接（FC）或时间通用表征，与叙事事件不对齐，可能丢失事件相关的预测信息。

Method: MovieSTAGE（Scene, Transition, Global Encoding）：场景内以超图结构组织FC profile，相邻场景间采用无符号FC-profile差异，并结合全片FC。在CMI-HBN Despicable Me队列260名被试上评估case-control、ADHD亚型及三分类任务，采用10次重复分层五折交叉验证、完整OOF预测、配对被试簇bootstrap与置换检验。

Result: 三类任务AUROC分别为0.69、0.73、0.75，平衡准确率分别为67.6%、69.8%、58.3%，均为所评估方法中最高均值点估计；三分类中完整模型优于所有两分支变体；匹配设置下HGNN场景编码器优于MLP、GAT、BNT；人工标注分区优于等时长随机与固定计数GSBS对照。

Conclusion: 受控结果表明，与全片FC结合时，事件对齐的场景与转换表征具有增量预测价值；事后建模分析提出涉及额顶与默认模式网络的网络层面假设。

Abstract: Naturalistic movie-fMRI provides a shared, temporally structured probe of brain dynamics, yet predictive models commonly rely on whole-run functional connectivity (FC) or temporally generic representations that are not aligned with narrative events. We introduce MovieSTAGE (Scene, Transition, and Global Encoding), a multiscale framework that combines hypergraph-structured FC-profile organization within scenes, unsigned FC-profile differences across adjacent scenes, and whole-movie FC. We evaluated 260 participants from the CMI-HBN Despicable Me cohort on case-control, ADHD-subtype, and three-class classification using 10 repetitions of stratified five-fold cross-validation, complete out-of-fold (OOF) predictions, and paired subject-cluster bootstrap and permutation tests. MovieSTAGE achieved AUROCs of 0.69, 0.73, and 0.75 and balanced accuracies of 67.6%, 69.8%, and 58.3%, respectively, yielding the highest mean point estimates among the evaluated methods. On the three-class task, the full model outperformed all two-branch variants, the HGNN scene encoder outperformed MLP, GAT, and BNT alternatives under matched settings, and the human-annotated partition outperformed duration-matched random and fixed-count GSBS controls. These controlled results support incremental predictive value from event-aligned scene and transition representations when combined with whole-movie FC in this cohort. Post-hoc model-derived analyses generated network-level hypotheses involving frontoparietal and default-mode systems.

</details>


### [75] [Denoising Blocks, Not Tokens: Efficient Compressed Continuous Diffusion with Branching Token Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09311)
*Xinsong Feng,Peng Du,Zhizhuo Yang,Daniel M. Bikel,Jiayun Wang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支潜在扩散（BLD），通过将长token序列压缩为极短的块潜变量序列，并在并行局部自回归分支上实现解码，从而大幅提升扩散语言模型的生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型（DLM）虽通过迭代并行细化生成文本，有望获得高于自回归（AR）解码的吞吐量，但多数模型仍保持"每token一个生成状态"，导致每次去噪步骤都要处理与输出序列等长的状态序列，限制了并行生成的吞吐收益。连续型DLM提供了额外自由度：单个连续状态可表示多个token，使扩散可在更短的潜序列上运行，从而释放效率潜力。

Method: 提出Branching Latent Diffusion（BLD）：（1）潜压缩，将1024-token序列压缩为仅64个块潜变量，实现16倍缩减；（2）分支token实现，每个潜变量由局部AR分支解码，所有分支并行运行；（3）分组生成，由于强压缩使联合潜生成困难，BLD按组生成潜变量，每组以已生成的潜变量为条件进行条件建模。

Result: 在同GPU端到端评估中，相较同等规模的ELF-L基线，BLD生成FLOPs减少80倍以上，吞吐量提升6倍以上；相较AR基线，吞吐量提升6倍以上，延迟降低4倍以上。尽管经过压缩，BLD仍保持有竞争力的局部流畅性与多样性，但长程连贯性仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: BLD表明，将扩散从token级状态迁移到压缩潜序列，可显著提升长序列生成的效率，为高效扩散语言建模提供了新方向，同时指出长程连贯性是未来需解决的关键问题。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text through iterative parallel refinement, offering the potential for higher throughput than autoregressive (AR) decoding. However, most DLMs still maintain one generative state per token, so every denoising step processes a state sequence as long as the output sequence, limiting the throughput gains from parallel generation. Continuous DLMs provide an additional degree of freedom: a single continuous state can represent multiple tokens, allowing diffusion to operate on a much shorter latent sequence. We introduce \emph{Branching Latent Diffusion (BLD)}, which exploits this flexibility by compressing a 1024-token sequence into only 64 block latents, a $16\times$ reduction. BLD combines latent compression with \emph{branching token realization}, where each latent is decoded by a local AR branch and all branches run in parallel. Because strong compression makes joint latent generation difficult, BLD generates the latents in groups, conditioning each group on previously generated latents. In end-to-end evaluation on the same GPU, BLD reduces generation FLOPs by more than $80\times$ and increases throughput by more than $6\times$ relative to the similarly sized ELF-L baseline. Compared with the AR baseline, BLD achieves more than $6\times$ higher throughput and more than $4\times$ lower latency. Despite the compression, BLD maintains competitive local fluency and diversity, although long-range coherence remains challenging. Overall, BLD shows that moving diffusion from token-level states to compressed latent sequences can substantially improve the efficiency of long-sequence generation.

</details>


### [76] [Benign Overfitting under Heterogeneous Input Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09340)
*Houzhen Liu,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究异质输入融合下最小范数线性插值的良性过拟合行为，发现其由输入交互产生的联合信号与谱几何决定，而非仅由边缘良性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性过拟合在学习单一高维输入时已被广泛研究，但其在异质输入融合（heterogeneous input fusion）下的行为尚未被探索。作者希望在保持总体任务固定的前提下，理解两个统计相关输入块的融合如何影响良性过拟合。

Method: 在异质高斯设计下研究最小范数线性插值（minimum-norm linear interpolation），比较两个统计相关的输入块及其融合。针对回归，引入全谱协方差证书（full-spectrum covariance certificate），并分析其对正半定联合协方差的保持性；针对一稀疏高斯分类，分析存活预测信号与干扰污染之间的平衡。

Result: 回归中识别出全谱协方差证书，其渐近状态与截断阈值无关，并被所有与两个边缘一致的正半定联合协方差保持；该保护是尖锐的，但并非所有良性回归问题都适用——在认证范围之外，两个良性边缘可产生有害融合。分类中良性由存活预测信号与干扰污染的平衡刻画，融合可使二者反向移动，且该类模型中所有边缘到联合的良性/非良性模式均可实现。同一融合输入对回归与分类可产生不同影响。

Conclusion: 异质融合下的良性过拟合由输入交互产生的联合信号与谱几何决定，而不是仅由边缘良性决定。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is extensively studied when learning from a single high-dimensional input, but its behavior under heterogeneous input fusion remains largely unexplored. We study this question for minimum-norm linear interpolation under a heterogeneous Gaussian design, comparing two statistically dependent input blocks with their fusion while holding the underlying population task fixed. For regression, we identify a full-spectrum covariance certificate whose asymptotic status is independent of the cutoff threshold and prove that it is preserved by every positive-semidefinite joint covariance consistent with the two marginals. This protection is sharp, yet it does not extend to all benign regression problems: outside the certified regime, two benign marginals can have a harmful fusion. For one-sparse Gaussian classification, benignity in the regular regime is characterized by the balance between surviving predictive signal and nuisance contamination. Fusion can move these two quantities in opposite directions, and within this model class every marginal-to-joint benign/non-benign pattern is attainable. We further show that the same fused input can have qualitatively different effects on regression and classification. These results establish that benign overfitting under heterogeneous fusion is determined by the joint signal and spectral geometry created by input interaction, rather than by marginal benignity alone.

</details>


### [77] [Shared Low-rank Basis Factorization for Data-free Mixture-of-Experts Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09342)
*Tianxiao Cao,Jiahe Shao,Yuning Qiu,Kyohei Atarashi,Hisashi Kashima,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析三类MoE压缩方法并证明剪枝与合并存在结构性误差，提出无数据的权重重建方法SLBF，通过专家间共享低秩基实现更优压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大模型参数量大，导致存储与部署困难，需要有效的压缩方法，但现有压缩方法（专家剪枝、合并、权重重建）各有局限，缺乏对其误差来源的理论分析。

Method: 首先从理论层面分析专家剪枝、专家合并与权重重建三类压缩方法，推导结构性误差界；基于分析结论，提出无数据的权重重建方法SLBF，在专家间共享秩为k的低秩基，并设计事后gauge fixing去除冗余参数。

Result: 理论分析表明剪枝与合并会产生与路由和专家异质性相关的非消失误差；权重重建可避免此类结构代价。SLBF在5种16B至122B参数的MoE架构上均优于三类压缩方法。

Conclusion: SLBF通过保留专家结构与路由、共享低秩基，实现更丰富的跨专家共享、更快收敛和更低重建误差，是优于剪枝与合并的MoE压缩方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models decouple capacity from compute through sparse routing, but their large parameter count creates storage and serving challenges. We analyze three MoE compression families: expert pruning, expert merging, and weight reconstruction, and derive structural error bounds showing that pruning and merging can incur non-vanishing errors tied to routing and expert heterogeneity. In contrast, weight reconstruction avoids these structural costs by preserving expert structure and routing. Motivated by the analysis, we propose Shared Low-rank Basis Factorization (SLBF), a data-free weight reconstruction method that uses rank-$k$ bases shared among experts, enabling richer cross-expert sharing, faster convergence, and lower reconstruction error. A post-hoc gauge fixing removes redundant parameters at no representational cost. Across five MoE architectures spanning 16B to 122B parameters, SLBF consistently outperforms methods from all three compression families.

</details>


### [78] [Multimodal LLMs Can Learn to Read Brain Signals: A Vision--Language Model for Unified Multi-Task EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09355)
*Parastoo Azizeddin,Omid Sharafi,Maryam M. Shanechi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BraVista，将多通道 EEG 编码为结构化图像，通过指令条件化的视觉-语言模型实现跨数据集多任务 EEG 解码，无需大规模 EEG 专用预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 表征跨认知任务、被试与记录条件的泛化仍是难题；基础模型虽提升解码性能，但"如何将神经信号有效接入这些模型以实现跨数据集多任务学习"这一关键问题尚未解决。

Method: BraVista 是视觉-语言框架：把多通道 EEG 信号编码为结构化图像，借助指令条件化的 VLM 完成多任务学习；采用对通用领域 VLM 的持续后训练，利用其视觉与语言先验适配神经信号，省去独立的 EEG 大规模预训练阶段。

Result: 在睡眠分期、情绪识别、认知负荷分类、异常 EEG 检测四个数据集上均取得较强性能；EEG 到图像的表示选择对性能影响显著；随噪声增强性能逐步下降，说明模型依赖 EEG 相关信息而非浅层视觉模式。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉表示是神经信号与通用基础模型之间有效且可扩展的接口，可支撑统一的多任务 EEG 解码。

Abstract: Learning EEG representations that generalize across cognitive tasks, subjects, and recording conditions remains a key challenge in electroencephalography (EEG) decoding. Recent advances in foundation models have improved EEG decoding performance, yet a fundamental open question remains: how to effectively interface neural signals with these models to enable multi-task learning across datasets. To investigate this question, we introduce BraVista, a visual-language framework that encodes multichannel EEG signals as structured images and enables multi-task learning through instruction-conditioned vision-language models (VLMs). Our approach relies on continued post-training of a general-domain VLM, leveraging its visual and linguistic priors to adapt to neural signals without a separate large-scale EEG-specific pretraining stage. We evaluate BraVista on four datasets spanning sleep staging, emotion recognition, cognitive workload classification, and abnormal EEG detection, showing strong performance across these tasks. Further analyses show that the choice of EEG-to-image representation is critical to performance. Moreover, through controlled perturbations of the EEG signal, we observe a gradual performance degradation under increasing noise, suggesting that the model relies on EEG-relevant information rather than superficial visual patterns. Together, these findings establish structured visual representations as an effective and scalable interface between neural signals and general-domain foundation models for unified multi-task EEG decoding.

</details>


### [79] [Global Exponential Convergence of Two-Layer Linear Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09356)
*Stephen Y Zhang,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为宽两层线性网络在光滑PL预测损失下的训练建立了定量收敛理论：将梯度流精确闭合为神经元分布协方差的有限维Bures流，利用均值场守恒律给出隐藏预条件块的谱下界，从而证明全局指数（线性）收敛率，并扩展到深度线性ResNet与重球动量情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽网络的均值场/特征学习理论虽已刻画训练动力学，但往往缺乏显式收敛率，且在奇异初始化、有限宽度、动量等实际设定下的定量保证不清楚。本文旨在给出显式速率的全局收敛证明，并说明其在有限宽度与动量下的稳健性。

Method: 以光滑Polyak-Lojasiewicz（PL）预测损失训练宽两层线性网络，令梯度流在因子层面精确闭合为神经元定律协方差的有限维Bures流，其中预测器动力学被隐藏协方差块预条件。借助均值场守恒律，在初始协方差满足谱支撑间隙条件时得到隐藏预条件块的一致谱下界（该条件包含正定但允许奇异初始化）。进一步将论证逐层推广至深度线性ResNet（受残差路径界约束），并对重球动量情形在位置-速度提升相协方差上建立闭合动力学。

Result: 对初始协方差Σ0=σ²Id，损失以至少4σ²κ的线性速率收敛到全局最小值（κ为PL常数）；证明该速率在有限宽度采样下的稳定性；给出因子梯度下降的显式步长区间（依赖光滑性、初始损失与守恒谱裕度）的全局收敛。对重球动量，线性收敛在能量与阻尼的显式条件下成立，给出可容许阻尼窗口；对两尺度白初始化，位置尺度足够大时该区间非空且在固定初始损失间隙与速度协方差下成立。数值实验刻画协方差几何并比较预测与观测速率。

Conclusion: 该工作为宽（深度）线性网络的训练提供了带显式速率的全局线性收敛保证，统一处理奇异初始化、有限宽度与动量加速，并通过奇异值/相协方差几何与实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We prove global exponential (linear) convergence with an explicit rate in the rich scaling for wide two-layer linear networks trained with smooth Polyak-Lojasiewicz predictor losses. Gradient flow in the factors closes exactly in terms of a finite-dimensional Bures flow of the neuron law covariance, in which the predictor dynamics are preconditioned by hidden covariance blocks. Mean-field conservation laws provide uniform spectral lower bounds on the hidden preconditioning blocks when the initial covariance satisfies a spectral support gap condition. This condition encompasses positive definiteness while still allowing for singular initializations. For an initial covariance $Σ_0 = σ^2 \mathrm{Id}$, the loss converges to the global minimum with linear rate at least $4σ^2κ$, where $κ$ is the PL constant. We establish stability of this rate under finite-width sampling, as well as global convergence of factor gradient descent for an explicit stepsize interval depending on smoothness, the initial loss, and conserved spectral margins. Our argument extends layerwise to deep linear ResNets, subject to a residual-path bound. In the case of heavy-ball momentum, training dynamics close instead over positions and velocities in terms of a lifted phase covariance. Linear convergence holds under an explicit condition on the energy and damping, specifying a window of admissible dampings. For two-scale white initializations, this interval is nonempty for sufficiently large position scales, with a fixed initial loss gap and velocity covariance. Numerical experiments illustrate the covariance geometry and compare the predicted and observed rates.

</details>


### [80] [From Retrieval to Customer Context: Evaluating Frontier-Model Systems for Voice-of-Customer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09375)
*Raviraja G,Viraj Bagal,Prabhath Chellingi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出"客户上下文图"（Customer Context Graph），将客户、运营及分析/行动对象以类型化关系统一建模，并与Agentic RAG、Deep Research Agent进行对比实验；在9,432条公开Cursor反馈与30个问题上，图谱支持的Agent总分0.961，显著优于Deep Research Agent（0.710）和Agentic RAG（0.651）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织日益依赖前沿语言模型分析客户反馈，但答案质量不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于反馈如何被组织与调用。现有方法（Agentic RAG、Deep Research Agent）缺乏对客户与业务上下文的统一结构化建模，难以回答"客户为什么这样说、谁受影响、采取了什么行动、谁负责、是否解决"等深层问题。

Method: 定义客户上下文图：以类型化关系连接客户对象（反馈、对话、用户、账户）、运营对象（工单、客服坐席、商机、竞品）和分析/行动对象（分类概念、证据、洞察、工作项、结果）。实验从公开Cursor反馈构建图谱，在9,432条记录、30个真实问题上对比Agentic RAG、Deep Research Agent与图谱支持的Agent；因无完备真值，采用比较式评分量表（在30题中的28题上校准），从答案质量（覆盖度与组织性）、分析深度（具体性与分解度）、证据质量（引用支持与可追溯性）三维度联合评分，并对引用记录进行抽样核验、三维度等权加权。

Result: 在统一量表下，图谱支持的Agent总分0.961，Deep Research Agent为0.710，Agentic RAG为0.651；在30个配对问题中27题领先Deep Research Agent（符号检验 p<10^-5，26/30为严格最优）。其最大优势体现在分析深度（0.967 vs 0.642），表现为更具体、层次化的发现，含量化主题与可追溯证据。

Conclusion: 以客户上下文图为核心的结构化上下文建模能显著提升语言模型分析客户反馈的答案质量、分析深度与证据可追溯性，优于纯检索与深度研究式Agent。该架构可推广至任意反馈来源，为客服洞察与行动闭环提供可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly use frontier language models to analyze customer feedback, but answer quality also depends on how that feedback is organized and made available. We define a \emph{customer context graph} as a unified model of customer and business context. Typed relationships connect customer objects (feedback, conversations, users, and accounts), operational objects (tickets, support agents, opportunities, and competitors), and analytical or action objects (taxonomy concepts, evidence, insights, work items, and outcomes). This lets an agent investigate not only what customers say, but why, who is affected, what action followed, who owns it, and whether it was resolved. For this experiment, the graph is populated from public Cursor feedback; the same architecture can support any type of feedback source. We compare Agentic RAG, a Deep Research Agent, and a Customer Context Graph-backed Agent on the same 9,432 public Cursor feedback records using 30 realistic product, incident, comparison, and metadata questions. Without exhaustive ground truth, we jointly score responses on answer quality (coverage and organization), analytical depth (specificity and decomposition), and evidence quality (citation support and traceability), using a comparative rubric calibrated on 28 of the 30 questions. We sample cited records against their claims and weight the three dimensions equally. Under this aligned rubric, the Customer Context Graph-backed Agent scores 0.961 overall, versus 0.710 for the Deep Research Agent and 0.651 for Agentic RAG, and leads the Deep Research Agent on 27 of 30 paired questions (sign-test p < 10^(-5); strictly best on 26 of 30). Its largest advantage is analytical depth (0.967 versus 0.642), reflecting more specific, hierarchically developed findings with quantified themes and traceable evidence...

</details>


### [81] [Noise, Denoise, Correct: MCMC Posterior Sampling with Diffusion Priors in Three Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09407)
*So Takao,Gregory David Bellchambers,Luke Ye,Sanmitra Ghosh,Michalis Michaelides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扩散华尔兹（diffusion waltz）：以SDEdit式加噪-去噪为提议分布，用Metropolis-Hastings校正实现精确后验采样，并借助无梯度集合卡尔曼更新将观测注入提议分布；在不可微Navier-Stokes初值反演任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型是求解逆问题的强大先验，但在非线性、不可微前向模型下的后验采样仍然困难。现有方法难以兼顾精确性与对不可微算子的适应性。

Method: 将SDEdit风格的加噪-去噪过程作为MCMC提议，使用Metropolis-Hastings校正以获得精确后验样本，且无需评估先验密度；进一步在不破坏精确性的前提下，用无梯度的集合卡尔曼更新把观测信息注入提议分布。

Result: 在不可微的Navier-Stokes初始条件恢复任务上，diffusion waltz在不同噪声水平与非线性程度下均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合MH校正的MCMC可在不可微、非线性前向算子下实现精确且有效的后验采样，观测注入进一步提升采样效率与精度。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models are powerful priors for inverse problems, but posterior sampling under nonlinear, non-differentiable forward models remain hard. We introduce diffusion waltz, an MCMC method using SDEdit-style noising-denoising as a proposal, corrected via Metropolis-Hastings for exact posterior sampling without prior evaluation. We further propose injecting observations into the proposal while preserving exactness, using a gradient-free ensemble Kalman update. On a non-differentiable Navier-Stokes initial condition recovery task, diffusion waltz outperforms existing baselines across different noise and nonlinearity regimes.

</details>


### [82] [Stability and Diversity of Networked Self-Consuming Generative Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09409)
*Xiukun Wei,Yang Zhang,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自消耗生成模型的递归训练问题推广到多模型网络化场景，用有向加权图建模模型间合成数据流动，分析重训练动态的长期收敛性与不动点，并考察真实数据可及性、跨模型数据消费和图结构对稳定性与多样性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI广泛部署使合成内容与真实数据难以区分，合成数据不可避免进入后续模型训练，形成自消耗训练循环。已有分析多局限于单模型自消耗或两模型简化交互，缺乏对多模型通过复杂交互路径互相消费合成数据的网络化场景的理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个理论框架：将各模型表示为有向加权图中的节点，边权刻画模型间合成数据的流动；在此框架下分析网络化模型在重训练动态下的长期行为，建立收敛条件并刻画相应的不动点。

Result: 建立了网络化自消耗模型重训练动态的收敛条件，刻画了系统达到的不动点，并揭示了系统长期稳定性与多样性如何受各模型对真实数据的可及性、跨模型数据消费以及交互图结构的影响。

Conclusion: 本文是理解网络化自消耗生成模型的第一步，所提图论框架为分析多模型递归训练下的收敛性、稳定性与多样性提供了理论基础，并表明真实数据获取、跨模型数据流与图结构是决定系统长期行为的关键因素。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of generative AI has made it increasingly difficult to distinguish synthetic content from real data. Consequently, synthetic data is inevitably incorporated into the training pipelines of future model generations, forming a self-consuming training loop. Prior work has studied the effects of such recursive self-consuming training, but analyses have largely been limited to isolated models, where a model consumes only its own synthetic data, or to simplified interactions between two models. This paper takes a first step toward understanding networked self-consuming generative models, in which multiple models consume synthetic data generated by one another through complex interaction pathways. We introduce a theoretical framework representing models as nodes in a directed, weighted graph, with edge weights governing the flow of synthetic data among models. Using this framework, we analyze the long-term behavior of networked models under retraining dynamics, establishing conditions for convergence and characterizing the resulting fixed points. We further investigate how the system's long-term stability and diversity are shaped by each model's access to real data, cross-model data consumption, and the structure of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [83] [Shared Geometry As A Rosetta Stone: Cross-Modal Alignment Without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09411)
*Dominik Schnaus,Thomas Dagès,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现跨模态对齐无需配对数据：提出 Wasserstein Procrustes 方法，通过粗略几何初始化估计单一正交映射，在无配对样本时可对齐独立训练的嵌入空间，且在极少配对场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表示支持零样本分类与检索，但传统跨模态对齐依赖大量配对数据。柏拉图表示假说（Platonic Representation Hypothesis）暗示不同模态独立训练的模型可能自发收敛到共享的表示几何结构。作者据此追问：跨模态对齐是否真的需要配对样本？

Method: 提出 Wasserstein Procrustes 方法：使用粗略几何初始化，在完全无配对样本的情况下估计单一正交映射，从而对齐两组不相交的嵌入集合；方法可自然扩展到少量配对样本的设定。

Result: 跨数据集、模态与单模态模型，方法可在无配对条件下稳定对齐独立训练表示，且标准几何对齐指标能准确预测何时可行；在极少配对样本下显著优于现有方法，在更多配对时与基于配对的方法保持竞争力；所得对齐可支持无配对样本的文本到图像生成。

Conclusion: 独立训练的模型往往共享足够的几何结构，使得在极少或零配对数据条件下即可建立跨模态对应关系，说明配对数据对粗粒度跨模态对齐并非必需。

Abstract: Multimodal representations enable zero-shot classification and retrieval, but aligning independently trained models usually requires large amounts of paired data. Yet, the Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that models trained on different modalities may converge spontaneously toward a shared representation geometry. But then, do we even need paired examples for cross-modal alignment? Remarkably, we show that paired examples are unnecessary for coarse cross-modal alignment. Our simple Wasserstein Procrustes method with a coarse geometric initialization aligns two disjoint embedding sets by estimating a single orthogonal map without seeing any pairs. Across datasets, modalities, and unimodal models, we show that we can consistently align independently trained representations without pairs, and standard geometric alignment metrics accurately predict when this is possible. Nevertheless, we can naturally benefit from paired examples. In the very few-pair regime, our method substantially outperforms existing ones, while staying competitive with pair-based methods with more added examples. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting alignments can enable text-to-image generation without paired examples. These results show that independently trained models often share enough geometry to establish cross-modal correspondence with little or no paired data.

</details>


### [84] [Self-Consuming Generative Models with Co-Evolving Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09415)
*Xiukun Wei,Tian Xie,Ding Zhu,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自消耗迭代训练中模型分布与用户偏好共同演化的耦合动力学：无参考数据时迭代策展放大初始偏差并收敛至单点均衡；注入足量参考数据则产生唯一全局吸引均衡，并据此提出联合选择参考分布与混合权重的高效控制算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作大多假设用户偏好固定，但实际中模型输出会逐渐重塑用户对"理想样本"的认知，形成模型分布与用户偏好共同演化的反馈回路。对这种耦合动态的长期行为缺乏理解。

Method: 将迭代策展建模为耦合动力系统：分析纯用户策展合成数据训练下的均衡结构，以及引入参考数据注入后的动力学变化；在此基础上提出联合优化参考分布与混合权重的算法以控制长期结果。

Result: 当训练完全依赖用户策展合成数据时，迭代策展放大初始偏差，系统收敛到多个单点均衡之一，初始占优的样本最终主导；当以足够大的速率注入参考数据时，动力学发生根本改变，得到唯一的全局吸引均衡。

Conclusion: 参考数据注入可有效控制耦合系统的长期结果；所提算法能在最小化数据收集成本的同时，将系统引导至保留所需属性的均衡。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly trained in self-consuming iterative loops, where users curate preferred samples from model-generated candidates and the curated samples are used to train future generations of the model. Prior work has largely assumed fixed user preferences, but in practice exposure to model outputs gradually reshapes what users perceive as desirable, creating a feedback loop in which model distributions and user preferences co-evolve. We take a first step toward understanding the long-term behavior of such coupled dynamics. We show that when training relies entirely on user-curated synthetic data, iterative curation amplifies initial biases and drives the system toward one of multiple singleton equilibria in which the instance holding an initial advantage eventually dominates. In contrast, injecting reference data into training at a sufficiently large rate fundamentally changes the dynamics and yields a unique globally attracting equilibrium. Building on this insight, we study how reference-data injection can be used to control long-term outcomes, and propose an efficient algorithm that jointly selects a reference distribution and its mixing weight to steer the coupled system toward equilibria that preserve desired attributes while minimizing data collection costs.

</details>


### [85] [What a Reporting Convention Hides: A Matched-Budget Audit of Quantum Natural Gradient with an Exactly Computed Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09425)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估"仅统计达标运行"与"单一目标判定"两种比较约定对变分量子优化器结论的影响，发现约定选择会改变Adam、SPSA与QNG之间的优劣判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的变分量子优化器比较常只对达到目标损失的运行计时，或仅在单一目标处读取结论；这两种约定都可能影响"代价更高的步骤是否值得"的判断，因此需要量化约定对结论的敏感性。

Method: 在从超参选择中留出的初始化上，比较Adam、SPSA与量子自然梯度（QNG）；精确计算QNG预条件所用度量，将每步折算为电路评估次数，并给所有方法相同预算；同时考察不同电路宽度、代价族与设置扫描的中位数。

Result: 在跨电路宽度、代价族与设置扫描的中位数池中，SPSA达到宽松目标所需评估次数超过Adam的两倍；剔除未达标的删失运行会掩盖这一差距。在全局代价族上固定为严格目标选定的设置后，QNG通常晚于Adam达到宽松目标，但率先达到严格目标；当把度量按参数数线性的模拟器代价替换为按参数数二次的假定硬件计数后，QNG的严格目标优势消失。

Conclusion: 建议对每个未达标运行也计入全部预算，并在多个目标上报告结论，以避免比较约定导致的偏差。

Abstract: Several published comparisons of variational quantum optimizers time only runs that reach a target loss, or read the verdict at a single target. Either convention could decide whether an optimizer's costlier steps pay off. We measure how much each convention changes verdicts among Adam, simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA) and quantum natural gradient (QNG), on initializations held out from the selection of settings. We compute the exact metric that preconditions QNG, price every step in circuit evaluations and give every method the same budget. In a median pooled over circuit widths, cost families and a sweep of settings with common misses, SPSA needs more than twice Adam's evaluations to reach a loose target. Dropping the censored runs that miss the target hides this gap. On the global-cost family we hold fixed the settings selected for a strict target. QNG then usually reaches the loose target after Adam but the strict target first. QNG's strict-target lead disappears when the metric's simulator price, linear in the number of parameters, is replaced by an assumed hardware count quadratic in that number. We recommend charging every miss the budget and reporting verdicts across targets.

</details>


### [86] [GeoPrior-Mamba: Structured Process Priors with Mamba for Fine-Resolution XCO2 Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09456)
*Zhao Meng,Yinan Cai,Siru Zhong,Juepeng Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoPrior-Mamba框架，利用语言模型离线构建结构化过程先验，并结合多方向Mamba骨干重建高分辨率XCO2场，RMSE降至0.81 ppm、R²达0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏卫星观测下重建精细分辨率XCO2场时，空间结构约束弱；现有学习方法将环境协变量视为普通数值输入，需从稀疏监督中学习异质源汇关系，难度大且收敛慢。

Method: 用语言模型在训练前将生物圈吸收、生态系统呼吸和人为排放的相对过程知识组织为确定性先验表；基于地理、生态、排放和季节信息进行空间实例化；通过轻量知识适配器自适应注入多方向Mamba重建骨干。

Result: 基于2018-2020年OCO-2观测，留出观测上RMSE为0.81 ppm、R²为0.93；相比CAMS背景插值RMSE降低48.2%，相比Trans-XCO2降低3.1%；消融显示知识先验分支有可测贡献且收敛显著加快；独立TCCON评估与地基柱CO2测量一致。

Conclusion: 语言模型可作为构建结构化过程先验的实用机制，尤其在难以直接获得全局一致过程-响应表示时，且该先验处于数值预测回路之外。

Abstract: Reconstructing fine-resolution column-averaged dry-air CO2 (XCO2) fields from sparse satellite observations requires models to infer spatial structure that is only weakly constrained by direct measurements. Existing learning-based methods typically treat environmental covariates as ordinary numerical inputs and must therefore learn heterogeneous source-sink relationships largely from sparse supervision. We introduce GeoPrior-Mamba, a multi-directional Mamba framework augmented with offline language-model-induced structured process priors. Rather than using a language model to predict XCO2, we use it before training to organize relative process knowledge for biospheric uptake, ecosystem respiration, and anthropogenic emissions into deterministic prior tables. These priors are spatially instantiated using geographic, ecological, emission-related, and seasonal information and are adaptively injected into the reconstruction backbone through a lightweight knowledge adapter. Using OCO-2 observations from 2018-2020, GeoPrior-Mamba achieves an RMSE of 0.81 ppm and an R2 of 0.93 on held-out observations, reducing RMSE by 48.2% relative to CAMS background interpolation and by 3.1% relative to Trans-XCO2 under the same evaluation protocol. Ablation experiments show a measurable contribution from the knowledge-prior branch and substantially faster convergence than the knowledge-free Mamba backbone. Independent TCCON evaluation further supports the consistency of the reconstructed fields with ground-based column CO2 measurements. These results suggest that language models can provide a practical mechanism for constructing structured process priors when globally consistent process-response representations are difficult to obtain directly, while remaining outside the numerical prediction loop.

</details>


### [87] [DSReg: Provably Recovering Individual World Latents without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09457)
*Yujia Zheng,David Klindt,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DSReg（依赖稀疏正则化），在无重建、无解码器、无标签条件下，借助"结构多样性"假设，从线性可辨识的 JEPA 表示中恢复个体世界潜变量（至符号置换），首次实现完全可辨识的 JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复个体潜变量的方法（非线性 ICA、字典学习、因果表示学习）依赖重建、辅助监督或非高斯性等分布不对称作为"锚点"；而无锚点方法（如 JEPA）只能辨识到线性变换，个体潜变量仍混合。作者希望弥合这一差距，实现无需重建、解码器和标签即可证明性恢复潜变量。

Method: 提出"结构多样性"（Structural Diversity）条件：不同潜变量在观测上留下不同的依赖足迹。在 LeJEPA 提供的线性可辨识性基础上，设计 DSReg（Dependency-Sparsity Regularization，依赖稀疏正则化），并将其事后（post hoc）应用于任意线性可辨识表示，可复用已训练检查点。

Result: 在结构多样性条件下，DSReg 可将个体世界潜变量恢复至符号置换，且无需重建或解码器；结构多样性严格弱于此前可辨识潜变量模型的所有结构条件。在合成数据、世界模型探针、学习到的视觉编码器及外部渲染器等实验中，DSReg 在保持密集预测性能的同时，随规模提升改善了个体潜变量恢复与下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个完全可辨识的 JEPA，能够在无重建、无解码器、无标签的情况下恢复每一个世界潜变量，并提供比现有方法更弱的识别条件，兼顾密集预测与个体潜变量恢复。

Abstract: Methods that recover individual latent variables of the world, from nonlinear ICA to dictionary learning and causal representation learning, anchor the latents to observations through reconstruction, auxiliary supervision, or distributional asymmetries such as non-Gaussianity. Methods without these anchors, including joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs), identify the latent state only up to a linear transformation, so individual latents remain mixed. We close this gap: individual world latents can be provably recovered with no reconstruction, no decoder, and no labels. The key condition is Structural Diversity: different latents leave distinct dependency footprints on observations, just as no two snowflakes are alike. Building on the linear identifiability that LeJEPA provides, we prove that under Structural Diversity, DSReg (Dependency-Sparsity Regularization) recovers individual world latents up to signed permutation, without reconstruction or a decoder. It applies post hoc to any linearly identified representation, reusing trained checkpoints at no loss over joint training, and establishes the first fully identifiable JEPA that recovers every world latent. Moreover, as a condition on dependency footprints, Structural Diversity is strictly weaker than all structural conditions of prior identifiable latent variable models. Across synthetic regimes, world model probes, learned visual encoders, and external renderers, DSReg preserves dense prediction while improving individual-latent recovery and downstream use with scales.

</details>


### [88] [When Should an In-Context Learner Expand Its Hypothesis Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09471)
*Weihan Li,Xinlei Chen,Junhao Wu,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"结构修订环境"（Structural Revision Environment），将"是否从熟悉模型族扩展到更丰富模型族"建模为代价敏感的序贯决策问题，揭示修订是价值边界而非证据阈值，并用Transformer与多种后训练语言模型检验该边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习系统在熟悉模型族内部适应很快，但更难的是更早一步的决策：面对可能是噪声、例外、族内变化或族外结构的观测，是否值得付出代价开启更丰富的模型族。作者试图把这一"预测失败→结构证据→扩展价值→行动"的链条形式化，并检验现有模型是否真正完成该推理。

Method: 构建Structural Revision Environment：为每种来源生成匹配的失败样本，独立变化扩展价格与剩余时间视野，支持精确贝叶斯计算与单次决策的精确规范解。在此环境中训练Transformer，并评估三个后训练谱系的语言模型；同时对元训练学习者进行受控后训练。

Result: 规范解显示最优行动随价格、视野和查询而变化，局部修复与扩展的边界由"规则预测不到的输入"决定，对丰富族的信念远早于决策发生。Transformer仅凭效用即可复现该边界。三类后训练语言模型在预测中携带失败敏感信号，但未反映于修订决策，且在有扩展收益时只读取收益而不与价格、视野权衡。允许推理的三模型能按参考比例权衡收益，但仍未将历史转化为"扩展能带来什么"的估计。受控后训练只移动先验和预测锐度，不移动判据。

Conclusion: 结构修订本质上是价值边界问题，而非证据阈值问题。现有语言模型在预测层面敏感于失败，却在决策层面缺失对价格、视野与扩展收益的正确权衡，后训练也难以改变这一判据，提示结构扩展决策能力仍存在系统性缺口。

Abstract: Learning systems adapt quickly inside a familiar family of models. The harder step comes earlier: deciding, from observations that could be noise, an exception, a change within the family or structure outside it, whether opening a richer family is worth its cost. We treat this as a costly sequential decision: prediction failure must be turned into structural evidence, evidence into a value of expansion, and value into action. The Structural Revision Environment produces matched failures from each source, varies the price of expansion and the remaining horizon independently of the evidence, and admits exact Bayesian calculations and an exact normative solution of the one-shot decision. Its solution shows that revision is a value boundary and not an evidence threshold: one history has different optimal actions under different prices, horizons and announced queries, the boundary between local repair and expansion is set by the inputs a rule predicts and a repair cannot cover, and belief in the richer family crosses long before the decision does. Transformers trained in the environment reproduce this boundary from utility alone. Language models of three post-training lineages carry a failure-sensitive signal in their predictions that is not reflected in their revision decisions, and given the gain of expanding they read it without weighing it against price and horizon. Three models allowed to reason weigh the stated gain in the reference's proportions and still do not turn the history into an estimate of what expansion would buy. Controlled post-training of the meta-trained learners moves the prior and the sharpness of predictions, and neither moves the criterion.

</details>


### [89] [MORA: Modeling Observed Changes for Drift-Robust Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09473)
*Xudong Mou,Tiejun Wang,Rui Wang,Hui Wang,Pin Liu,Tianyu Wo,Xudong Liu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出时间序列异常检测中的"时间变化消歧"问题（区分局部偏差是异常还是分布漂移），并提出MORA框架：用短期与长期双视图重建同一局部目标，以重建差距衡量上下文支撑度，并用数据依赖的校正机制保守地调整局部异常分。无需漂移标注与在线自适应，在四个基准上兼顾非平稳鲁棒性与真实异常敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境下，分布漂移与真实异常会产生相似的局部变化，导致难以判断偏差究竟代表异常还是上下文的演化。现有方法或适应检测到的漂移、或学习对漂移不敏感的表示，均未真正解决这一歧义。作者将此问题定义为"时间变化消歧"。

Method: MORA框架：从配对的短期视图与长期视图重建同一局部目标；以两者重建差距度量局部偏差所获得的上下文支撑；引入数据依赖的校正机制，对主局部异常分数进行保守调整，仅当上下文能改善同一目标的重建时才降低分数。方法不需要漂移标注，也不需要在线自适应。

Result: 在四个时间序列异常检测基准上的实验表明，MORA在非平稳条件下具有较强鲁棒性，同时保持对真实异常的检测敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过双视图重建差距与保守校正，MORA在不依赖漂移标注和在线自适应的前提下缓解了分布漂移与异常混淆问题，为TSAD提供了一种消歧式的漂移鲁棒方案。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) identifies deviations from patterns learned from historical data. In non-stationary settings, distribution drift and true anomalies can cause similar local changes, making it difficult to tell whether a deviation reflects abnormality or evolving context. Existing methods typically adapt to detected shifts or learn drift-insensitive representations, but do not resolve this ambiguity. We define this problem as \emph{temporal change disambiguation}: determining whether a local deviation is explained by broader temporal evolution. We introduce MORA, a drift-robust TSAD framework that reconstructs the same local target from paired short- and long-term views. The reconstruction gap measures contextual support for a local deviation, and a data-dependent correction mechanism conservatively adjusts the primary local anomaly score. Context can only reduce the score when it improves reconstruction of the same target. MORA needs neither drift annotations nor online adaptation. Experiments on four TSAD benchmarks show strong robustness to non-stationarity while preserving sensitivity to genuine anomalies.

</details>


### [90] [CHASE: Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation for Geometry-Aware Model Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09476)
*Wei Wang,Wei Jiang,Ziran Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CHASE 框架，将几何与谱对齐（GSA）所刻画的结构（谱集中、物理通道对齐、支撑结构、奇异基变化）直接用于模型设计，覆盖六类应用（修改、重配置、压缩），包括 CORA、COEC、CORAM、CAGA、SAKV、CAPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GSA 已能刻画训练网络的谱集中、物理通道对齐、支撑结构和奇异基变化等结构性质，但这些结构此前主要用于分析，缺乏将其直接转化为实用模型设计与操作方法的工作。

Method: 提出 CHASE（Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation）框架，统一利用 GSA 结构。其中 CORA、COEC、CORAM 分别将 GSA 应用于参数高效微调、结构化剪枝补偿与模型融合；另提出三种新方法：CAGA 通过几何对齐与低秩子空间提取识别可共享 KV 表示的注意力头并构造共享 K/V 头；SAKV 判断哪些相邻层可共享低秩 KV-cache 表示并确定各层组保留秩；CAPS 依据谱结构对输出神经元分组，并为每组分别选择保留的输入通道。

Result: CORA、COEC、CORAM 的结果验证了 GSA 在自适应、剪枝补偿与模型融合上的有效性；CAGA 实验显示几何共享头构造显著改善 MHA 到 GQA 的转换；SAKV 与 CAPS 在 KV-cache 压缩和结构化剪枝上优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: GSA 所识别的结构可直接用于设计覆盖多种模型操作的实用方法，表明结构与方法设计之间存在可操作的直接联系。

Abstract: Geometric and Spectral Alignment (GSA) characterizes trained networks through spectral concentration, physical-channel alignment, support structure, and changes in singular bases. In this paper, we propose CHASE (Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation) to use these structures in practical model design. CHASE covers six applications across model modification, reconfiguration, and compression. CORA, COEC, and CORAM apply GSA to parameter-efficient finetuning, structured-pruning compensation, and model merging. We further develop three new methods. CAGA uses GSA to identify multi-head attention heads that can share a KV representation and constructs the shared key and value heads through geometric alignment and low-rank subspace extraction. SAKV uses GSA to determine which adjacent layers can share a low-rank KV-cache representation and the retained rank for each layer group. CAPS uses GSA spectral structure to group output neurons and selects retained input channels separately for each group. Results from CORA, COEC, and CORAM establish the effectiveness of GSA for adaptation, pruning compensation, and model merging. Experiments on CAGA show that geometric shared-head construction substantially improves MHA-to-GQA conversion, and SAKV and CAPS improve over representative baselines for KV-cache compression and structured pruning. These results show that the structures identified by GSA can be used directly to design methods for a range of model operations.

</details>


### [91] [Safe on Average, Unsafe in the Tail: When Is the Episodic-Cost Tail Controllable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09508)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出主流安全强化学习仅约束期望回合成本，无法刻画回合间成本分布，因此提出用CVaR_0.1度量最差10%回合的平均成本来定义"尾部安全"，并在Safety-Gymnasium任务上识别平均安全但尾部不安全的策略，进而研究在保持回报的前提下控制尾部违规的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法大多在期望回合成本上施加约束，标准评估也只报告平均回合成本，不刻画成本在回合间的分布。这使得满足平均成本约束的策略在最差回合中仍可能不安全，而仅凭均值报告既无法识别这种尾部违规，也无法判断是否能在保持回报的同时将其控制在预算内。

Method: 用CVaR_0.1（最差10%回合的平均成本）度量回合成本的尾部，当CVaR_0.1处于安全预算内时将该策略判定为尾部安全。在三个Safety-Gymnasium导航任务上评估五种标准算法以识别尾部不安全的策略；随后在密集危险导航环境中考察四类约束族，并在四个导航任务与四个运动任务上评估尾部控制效果。

Result: 在三个Safety-Gymnasium导航任务上，识别出存在平均安全但尾部不安全的策略；在密集危险导航中比较了四类约束族的表现，并在四个导航与四个运动任务上给出了尾部控制的结果。

Conclusion: 仅以期望回合成本为约束和评价标准不足以保障安全性，需要引入CVaR等尾部风险指标来刻画回合成本分布。用CVaR_0.1定义尾部安全可揭示平均安全策略的潜在尾部违规，并为在保持回报的同时控制尾部风险提供可行的评估框架。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning seeks policies that maximize return while satisfying constraints on cumulative cost. Most methods impose these constraints on expected episodic cost. Consequently, standard evaluations report mean episodic cost without characterizing how cost is distributed across episodes. A policy that satisfies the mean-cost criterion may therefore remain unsafe in its worst episodes. Mean-cost reporting neither identifies this tail violation nor shows whether it can be brought within budget while preserving return. In this work, we measure the episodic-cost tail using $\mathrm{CVaR}_{0.1}$, the average cost of the worst $10\%$ of episodes. We classify a policy as tail-safe when $\mathrm{CVaR}_{0.1}$ is within the safety budget. This allows us first to identify policies that are safe on average but unsafe in the tail and then to study whether their tail violations can be controlled while preserving return. To identify tail-unsafe policies, we evaluate five standard algorithms on three Safety-Gymnasium navigation tasks. We then examine four constraint families on dense-hazard navigation and assess tail control across four navigation and four locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Differential Refresh Policies for Models Trained on Lagging Data Snapshots: From a Single-Age Equivalence Limit to an Optimal Per-Segment Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09519)
*Amit Rajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出自适应刷新触发（全局陈旧度评分）与「校准的均匀年龄定时器」等价，无法携带额外调度信息；真正的杠杆是「差异化分段刷新」，并按预算分配求解最优刷新率（∝ √(w_j λ_j)），在匹配预算下优于均匀定时器 8–29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的 ML 模型是基于时间受限训练快照产出的 artifact，一经部署即开始「变旧」。业界常用做法是用自适应触发（对来源、分段、敏感度加权的陈旧度评分，累积风险超阈值即重训）取代固定重训节奏，但论文要论证这一「杠杆」选错了，并寻找真正有效的杠杆。

Method: 1) 提出「等价性极限（equivalence limit）」：任何作为单一共享全局训练数据年龄的静态严格单调函数的刷新触发，在操作上等价于一个校准过的均匀年龄定时器；因此全局陈旧度预算（无论赋权多精细）不携带时钟之外的调度信息。2) 由此导出逃逸路径：对分段做差异化刷新，使各分段拥有各自的年龄与刷新间隔，这要求刷新成本在分段间可分离（增量训练或按分段的模型）。3) 求解预算分配问题，在频繁刷新体制下得到每分段最优刷新率 ∝ √(w_j λ_j)（权重 × 变化率），并给出闭式 Cauchy–Schwarz「均匀性代价（price of uniformity）」。4) 用真实 Poisson 变化事件的离散事件仿真验证，并与均匀定时器及朴素暴露阈值策略对比，再加入 50% 速率估计噪声做鲁棒性检验。

Result: 最优策略在匹配刷新预算下相较均匀定时器将实际加权陈旧暴露降低 8–29%，在 86–100% 的随机种子（seeds）上胜出；朴素暴露阈值策略做不到这一点，说明收益来自「分配」而非「评分」；该优势在 50% 速率估计噪声下依然保持。最优策略的成本从不超过均匀定时器，其超出量由零（同质负载）随异质性增长（异质负载）的闭式 Cauchy–Schwarz 代价刻画。

Conclusion: 模型刷新中的杠杆不在于设计更好的陈旧度评分（更好的 score），而在于采取更好的行动（更好的 action）：即用可分离成本的分段差异化刷新替代全局陈旧度触发的均匀化效果，并按 √(w_j λ_j) 分配刷新预算。

Abstract: Production machine-learning models are derived artifacts of time-bounded training snapshots: a deployed model is a materialized view over a training cut that ages the instant it is built. A common response is to replace the fixed retraining cadence with an adaptive trigger -- a weighted staleness score that retrains when accumulated source risk crosses a threshold. We show this is the wrong lever, and identify the right one. First, an equivalence limit: any refresh trigger that is a static, strictly monotone function of a single shared global training-data age is operationally equivalent to a calibrated uniform age timer, so a global staleness budget, however elaborately it weights segments, sources, and sensitivities, carries no scheduling information a clock does not. The limit also shows how to escape it: refresh segments differentially, giving each its own age and refresh interval, which is meaningful when refresh cost is separable across segments (incremental training or per-segment models). We solve the resulting budget-allocation problem. In the frequent-refresh regime each segment's optimal refresh rate is proportional to the square root of its risk $w_j λ_j$ (weight times change rate), and the optimal policy never costs more than the uniform timer, beating it by a closed-form Cauchy-Schwarz "price of uniformity" that is zero for homogeneous workloads and grows with heterogeneity. In a discrete-event simulation with real Poisson change events, the optimal policy lowers realized weighted stale exposure by 8-29% relative to the uniform timer at matched refresh budget, winning on 86-100% of seeds; a naive exposure-threshold policy does not, showing the allocation is what helps; and the advantage survives 50% rate-estimation noise. The leverage in model refresh is not a better score but a better action.

</details>


### [93] [Align Before You Combine: Reference Space Calibration for Supervision Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09525)
*Jackson Eshbaugh,Jorge Silveyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"校准优先"框架，在无真实标签、无共享标注空间条件下生成监督分数：先用有序校准特征构建合成序数参考空间，将各子集评分器对齐到统一尺度后再融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏真实标签（ground-truth labels）与共享标注空间（shared annotation space）时，各子集评分器输出尺度不可比，直接平均融合不可靠，需要一种与训练集经验分布无关的对齐机制。现有依赖样本的校准方法受训练集分布影响，泛化性受限。

Method: 校准优先框架：基于代表潜在概念的有序校准特征（ordered calibration features）构建合成序数参考空间（synthetic ordinal reference space）；在该参考空间上对子集特定评分器（subset-specific scorers）进行对齐，而非使用训练样本，从而与训练集经验分布独立；对齐后再做融合（fusion）。同时探究降低每个特征的校准层级数（calibration levels），以低计算成本逼近高分辨率结果。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，该框架稳定优于未校准的平均（uncalibrated averaging），且主要指标点估计高于最优单个评分器。与依赖样本的基线相比，性能差异随领域变化：Ames Housing 绝对差小于0.02，Breast Cancer Wisconsin 与 Wine Quality 小于0.01。经 Bonferroni 校正后，Ames Housing 三项比较均显著，Breast Cancer Wisconsin 有一项显著。减少校准层级可在显著降低计算成本的同时近似高分辨率结果。

Conclusion: 该框架在既无真实标签又无共享标注空间的场景下，可作为构建监督分数的可行方案；校准层级数与计算成本间存在可调权衡。

Abstract: We introduce a calibration-first framework that produces supervision scores without access to ground-truth labels or a shared annotation space. Our framework aligns subset-specific scorers using a synthetic ordinal reference space before fusion. This reference space is constructed from ordered calibration features that represent the latent concept, providing a common scale on which otherwise incomparable scorer outputs can be aligned. Because our calibration procedure uses the reference space rather than training samples, it is independent of the training set's empirical distribution. Across three benchmark datasets, our framework consistently outperforms uncalibrated averaging and achieves higher primary-metric point estimates on the evaluation metrics than the best individual scorer. Performance relative to sample-dependent baselines varies by domain, with absolute differences below 0.02 on Ames Housing and below 0.01 on Breast Cancer Wisconsin and Wine Quality. After Bonferroni correction, differences remain significant for all three comparisons on Ames Housing and one on Breast Cancer Wisconsin. Additionally, we show that using fewer calibration levels per feature can closely approximate higher-resolution results at substantially lower computational cost. Together, these results support our framework as a viable approach to construct supervision scores when neither ground-truth labels nor a shared annotation space is available.

</details>


### [94] [CircuitGate: Logic-Consistent Circuit-Level Functional Modeling for And-Inverter Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09549)
*Qifan Zhang,Ruijie Li,Fangzhou Zhang,Qian Ma,Hui Li,Furui Zhan,Yongpeng Wang,Liying Hao,Shikai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CircuitGate，一种功能感知的 AIG 表示学习框架，从门级语义迈向电路级功能建模，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 GNN 的 AIG 表示方法主要依赖局部门级消息传递，难以捕获电路级功能上下文，且学到的表示对拓扑特定模式敏感，缺乏对功能保持变换的鲁棒性。

Method: CircuitGate 显式编码全局主输入（PI）支撑集，建模扇入间支撑重叠感知的重汇聚关系，并引入逻辑启发的布尔约束以促使表示功能一致。

Result: 在 ForgeEDA、EPFL 和 ITC'99 基准上评估，在等价门识别与信号概率预测任务中持续优于现有方法，MAE 分别最多降低 21.7% 和 14.2%；在无微调的 ForgeEDA 到 OpenABC 直接迁移下，等价门识别取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 建模超越局部拓扑的电路级功能依赖关系是有效的，CircuitGate 具备强跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: And-Inverter Graphs (AIGs) are fundamental representations for logic synthesis and verification in Electronic Design Automation (EDA). As structured representations of complex digital systems, AIGs require models to capture functional dependencies beyond local structure and remain robust to functionality-preserving transformations. In learning-based AIG representation, existing approaches are predominantly based on GNNs and rely on local gate-level message passing, limiting their ability to capture circuit-level functional context and making the learned representations sensitive to topology-specific patterns. Therefore, we propose CircuitGate, a function-aware AIG representation learning framework that advances from gate-level semantics to circuit-level functional modeling. CircuitGate explicitly encodes global primary-input (PI) support and models support-overlap-aware reconvergence between fanins, while incorporating logic-inspired Boolean constraints to encourage functionally consistent representations. We evaluate CircuitGate on the large-scale ForgeEDA benchmark and further validate it on the EPFL and ITC'99 benchmarks. Across equivalent-gate identification and signal-probability prediction tasks, CircuitGate consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving up to 21.7% and 14.2% reductions in MAE, respectively. Under direct ForgeEDA-to-OpenABC transfer without fine-tuning, CircuitGate also achieves the best equivalent-gate identification performance, demonstrating strong cross-dataset generalization. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of modeling circuit-level functional dependencies beyond local topology.

</details>


### [95] [A Framework for the Systematic Review of ML Assets in AI Registries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09551)
*Alexandra González,Quim Motger,Xavier Franch,Silverio Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将系统综述方法从科学文献迁移至 AI 注册库，提出以 ML 资产（预训练模型、数据集、基准）为一级分析单元的结构化检索框架，涵盖注册库感知搜索、跨库模式对齐与依赖驱动探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统日益依赖 ML 资产进行构建、评估与集成，但当前的资产探索、选择与复用缺乏类似传统证据综合的系统化检索方法。实践中，ML 资产选择常被视为既定设计决策，仅凭非正式理由支撑，缺乏可追溯、基于证据且可更新的选择过程。

Method: 分析科学文献中已确立的系统综述实践，将其阶段（规划、执行、记录）适配到 AI 注册库场景，并借鉴若干关键技术手段：注册库感知的搜索策略、跨注册库的模式对齐，以及依赖驱动建模与探索。

Result: 将 ML 资产检索概念化为系统化、可复现的过程而非临时性活动，并提出一个用于结构化 ML 资产发现的框架。

Conclusion: 该工作表明系统综述原则可超越科学文献范畴，用于支持面向持续演进 AI 注册库的证据综合，从而提升 ML 资产选择的透明度与可复现性。

Abstract: Background: Modern software systems increasingly rely on Machine Learning (ML) assets (i.e., pre-trained models, datasets, benchmarks) for building, evaluating, and integrating ML-based systems. However, current exploration, selection and reuse practices of ML assets are not supported by systematic retrieval methodologies comparable to those used in traditional evidence synthesis. Consequently, in practice, ML asset selection is often presented as a settled design decision, supported by informal justification rather than a traceable, evidence-based, and updatable selection process. Aims: This paper explores how systematic review methods can support ML asset retrieval. In doing so, we aim to make their selection transparent and reproducible, grounded in explicit evidence, and ultimately better suited to its intended use. Method: We analyze established systematic review practices from scientific literature and adapt their phases (i.e., planning, conducting, and documenting) to Artificial Intelligence (AI) registries, treating ML assets as first-class units of analysis. The resulting framework integrates registry-aware search strategies, cross-registry schema alignment, and dependency-driven ML asset exploration. Results: We conceptualize ML asset retrieval as a systematic and reproducible process rather than an ad hoc activity, and propose a framework for structured ML asset discovery. \textbf{Conclusions:} This work illustrates how systematic review principles can be extended beyond scientific literature to support evidence synthesis over evolving AI registries.

</details>


### [96] [UniCSI Towards a Universal Wi-Fi CSI Encoder for Ubiquitous Human Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09559)
*Daniel Eckhoff,Hua Kang,Zhitang Chen,Jie Chuai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 UniCSI 统一基础架构，直接处理异构 CSI 信号，通过物理感知 RF 分词器与频谱聚合器实现跨配置、跨域建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi 感知可利用现有无线信号实现普适人体感知，但 CSI 采集受设备配置（子载波数、带宽、频段）差异影响，导致张量形状与频谱分辨率异构，标准架构难以直接建模，常需有损预处理而破坏原始信号。

Method: UniCSI 包含两项核心设计：(1) 物理感知 RF 分词器，按频率在物理频谱中的分数位置而非固定数组索引编码每个频信道，保持内在频谱相干性，实现与频率分辨率无关的异构处理；(2) 频谱聚合器，将变长信道序列蒸馏为固定大小频谱签名，使特征维度与子载波间距解耦。

Result: 在 25 个异构公开数据集（14–2048 子载波、20–160 MHz 带宽、2.4/5 GHz 频段）的大规模评测中，原生异构输入在监督与自监督训练下均显著提升跨域迁移，尤其在固定网格架构难以泛化的场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: UniCSI 作为统一基础架构，可保留原始波形完整性并直接处理异构 CSI，为跨设备配置的 Wi-Fi 感知提供可泛化的建模范式。

Abstract: Wi-Fi sensing promises to turn the everyday wireless signals that already surround us into ubiquitous sensors for human sensing. However, a fundamental obstacle is that CSI is acquired under diverse device-specific configurations, including different subcarrier counts, bandwidths, and carrier bands. Consequently, the resulting CSI tensors vary in both spectral resolution and tensor shape, making heterogeneous modeling challenging. Standard architectures struggle with such heterogeneity, forcing lossy pre-processing which compromises the underlying signal. To bridge this gap, we present UniCSI, a unified foundation architecture that directly operates on heterogeneous CSI while preserving the integrity of the native waveform. UniCSI hinges on two core innovations: (1) a physics-informed RF tokenizer that encodes each frequency channel based on its fractional position within the physical spectrum rather than rigid array indices. It preserves intrinsic spectral coherence and enables seamless, frequency resolution-agnostic processing across arbitrary sensing configurations. (2) a spectral aggregator that distills variable-length channel sequences into a fixed-size spectral signature, effectively decoupling the feature dimensionality from the physical subcarrier spacing. Extensive evaluations on a large-scale corpus of 25 heterogeneous public datasets, spanning 14 to 2048 subcarriers, 20 to 160 MHz bandwidth, and the 2.4 and 5 GHz bands, demonstrate that native heterogeneous ingestion substantially improves cross-domain transfer under both supervised and self-supervised training schemes, particularly in regimes where fixed-grid architectures fail to generalize.

</details>


### [97] [EvoSignal: LLM-Guided Evolutionary Design of Modular Traffic Signal Control Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09563)
*Leizhen Wang,Peibo Duan,Zhenlin Qin,Yancheng Ling,Jian Xu,Yue Wang,Hao Wang,Zhenliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将交通信号控制（TSC）建模为模块化程序设计问题，提出LLM引导的进化框架EvoSignal，通过交通知识与性能反馈自动改进信号控制程序，无需在线LLM推理，在多场景下显著优于20个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效TSC需响应动态交通需求与网络条件并满足多种控制目标，但现有策略适配依赖反复人工设计与调整，难以系统探索更优控制规则；直接用LLM选择信号相位会导致决策规则黑箱化并带来持续推理成本与延迟。

Method: 将TSC形式化为模块化程序设计问题，模块表示分离交通特征提取、局部相位优先级排序及可选的基于网络优先级的调整；从若干已有策略出发，利用LLM结合交通知识与拥堵、信号运行性能反馈进行进化搜索，保留具有不同性能权衡的策略；生成的程序运行时无需在线LLM推理。

Result: 在两个真实路网的五个场景中，选定的默认EvoSignal程序相较每个场景中20个传统、强化学习及LLM基线的最低等待时间降低16.8%–49.2%；一个优先考虑旅行时间与排队长度的程序在搜索场景三个指标上均超越全部20个基线，并无改动迁移到其余四个场景时各指标仍保持前三。

Conclusion: 研究支持可检查控制程序的自动化设计，且这些程序在所评估的路网与交通需求间具备可迁移性。

Abstract: Effective traffic signal control (TSC) requires policies that respond to changing traffic demand and network conditions while meeting different control objectives. However, adapting existing strategies often involves repeated manual design and adjustment, making it difficult to systematically explore better control rules for a target network. Large language models (LLMs) can automate this process, but directly using them to select signal phases leaves decision rules embedded in black-box models and incurs recurring inference costs and latency. This paper formulates TSC as a modular program design problem and proposes EvoSignal, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework using traffic knowledge and performance feedback. The modular representation separates traffic feature extraction, local phase prioritization, and optional network-based priority adjustment. Starting from several established strategies, EvoSignal improves programs through feedback on congestion and signal operation, retaining strategies with different performance trade-offs. The resulting programs operate without online LLM inference. Simulation experiments across five scenarios on two real-world road networks show that the selected default EvoSignal program reduces waiting time by 16.8--49.2\% relative to the lowest waiting time achieved by the 20 conventional, reinforcement learning-based, and LLM-based baselines in each scenario. A program prioritizing travel time and queue length outperforms all 20 baselines on all three metrics in the search scenario and remains among the top three on each metric when transferred unchanged to the other four scenarios. These findings support automated design of inspectable control programs that transfer across the evaluated road networks and traffic demands.Code is available at https://github.com/georgewanglz2019/EvoSignal.

</details>


### [98] [Online Resource Allocation with an Endogenous Markov State: Fewer LP Solves Earn More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09577)
*Zhaohua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有限时域在线资源分配（i.i.d.请求+内生马尔可夫状态），以瞬态流体LP为界、稳态LP给出随机化状态相关控制；给出频繁/不频繁重解的O(1)与Θ(√T)等最优率，并提出未知先验下的三相U形低重解频率策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线资源分配与重解（re-solving）研究多假设状态外生或缺乏对“重解频率”的渐近刻画；本文关注行动影响状态转移的内生马尔可夫状态，并试图澄清频繁优化是否总优于不频繁优化，以及未知请求先验下的学习-库存修正协调问题。

Method: 建立瞬态流体LP上界基准与稳态LP随机化状态相关控制；在稳态LP唯一最优下引入原问题非退化（primal nondegeneracy）与最优诱导核不可约（irreducibility）作为正则条件。已知先验时，分析频繁/不频繁重解在非退化与退化最优下的遗憾。未知先验时，设计三相U形不频繁重解策略，协调学习与库存修正，仅需O(log log T)次LP求解；并在给定最优目标状态类与常数代价进入策略时给出保证。还进行数值实验比较逐轮重解与按纪元重解、阈值化效果等。

Result: 已知先验：在非退化且不可约下，频繁与不频繁重解均达到O(1)遗憾；退化最优下，不可约使不频繁重解的最坏情形率精确为Θ(√T)，而频繁重解可能达到Ω(T)。未知先验：三相U形不频繁重解在不可约、给定最优目标状态类与常数代价进入策略时，非退化O(1)、退化O(√T)；缺少目标类信息时线性极小极大遗憾不可避免。数值实验显示逐轮重解不稳定、按纪要不频繁重解更稳，阈值化显著缓解损失，且不频繁方案在已知与估计先验下均占优。

Conclusion: 在内生马尔可夫状态的有限时域在线资源分配中，重解频率并非越高越好：退化最优下频繁重解可能遭受Ω(T)遗憾，而不频繁重解可达Θ(√T)甚至O(1)。原问题非退化与最优诱导核不可约是决定遗憾率的关键正则条件；未知先验下，采用O(log log T)次LP求解的三相U形不频繁重解策略可在适当信息条件下实现最优或近最优遗憾，而目标状态类信息对避免线性遗憾至关重要。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon online resource allocation with i.i.d. requests and an endogenous Markov state on a finite state space: each action affects the transition of the state that governs future rewards and resource consumption. In this problem, a transient fluid LP benchmark upper bounds the expected reward of every nonanticipating policy, while a stationary LP supplies randomized state-dependent controls. We assume that the stationary LP has a unique optimum and identify primal nondegeneracy and irreducibility of the optimal induced kernel as important regularity conditions in this framework. With a known request prior, we show that, under nondegeneracy and irreducibility, both frequent and infrequent re-solving attain $O(1)$ regret. However, under a degenerate optimum, irreducibility yields the sharp worst-case $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ rate for infrequent re-solving, while frequent re-solving can incur $Ω(T)$ regret. Thus, more frequent optimization can perform asymptotically worse. With an unknown request prior, we develop a three-phase U-shaped infrequent re-solving policy that coordinates learning and inventory correction with $O(\log\log T)$ LP solves. When the optimal induced kernel is irreducible and the algorithm is given the optimal target state class and a constant-cost entrance policy, it attains $O(1)$ regret under nondegeneracy and $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret under degeneracy. Without the target-class information, linear minimax regret is unavoidable. Numerical experiments further illustrate the instability of round-by-round re-solving relative to epoch-wise infrequent re-solving, show that thresholding greatly mitigates its loss, and find that infrequent schemes remain dominant under both known and estimated priors.

</details>


### [99] [Collaborative Reasoning Distillation via Cross-Feedback and Coherent Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09587)
*Taehoon Kim,Seunggeun Cho,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出协作推理蒸馏（CRD）框架，通过教师交互式交叉反馈、细粒度逐步质量评估和连贯性感知步骤拼接三项创新，配合预算约束下的推理质量优化（RQO），显著提升小模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提升大语言模型推理能力的方法要么需要巨大算力预算，要么难以有效将推理能力蒸馏到小模型；且标准蒸馏方法依赖结果导向奖励，无法区分扎实推理与侥幸猜测。

Method: CRD框架包含三项创新：(1) 交互式交叉反馈，教师相互迭代批判彼此推理；(2) 细粒度逐步质量评估，独立于最终答案评估逻辑有效性；(3) 连贯性感知步骤拼接，综合互补优势。学生模型通过预算约束下的推理质量优化（RQO）进行训练。

Result: CRD-4B在MATH-500上达到97.3%，在AIME'25上达到70.3%，超越基线，且仅使用5万训练样本，比同类模型数据集小至多12倍。

Conclusion: CRD以更小的数据规模实现了优越的推理性能，证明通过过程导向的步骤级质量评估与教师协作反馈可高效蒸馏推理能力至紧凑模型。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are critical for advancing Large Language Models, yet current approaches either require massive computational budgets or struggle to effectively distill reasoning to smaller models. Standard distillation methods rely on outcome-based rewards, failing to distinguish between sound reasoning and lucky guesses. We propose Collaborative Reasoning Distillation (CRD), a framework that enhances reasoning in compact models through three innovations: (1) interactive cross-feedback where teachers iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (2) fine-grained step-wise quality assessment capturing logical validity independent of final answers, and (3) coherence-aware step stitching that synthesizes complementary strengths. Students are trained via Reasoning Quality Optimization (RQO) with budget constraints. Our model, CRD-4B, achieves 97.3% on MATH-500 and 70.3% on AIME'25, surpassing baselines while using only 50K training examples, up to 12 times smaller than the datasets of comparable models.

</details>


### [100] [COPC: Coupled Off-Policy Correction for Asynchronous LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09597)
*Zicheng Hu,Zhijian Zhou,Xuan Zhang,Yuchen Liu,Cheng Chen,Yuan Li,Qi Gu,Yan Feng,Hongyan Hao,Chao Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示异步强化学习中"优势陈旧性"问题，提出联合修正策略与优势两侧误差的COPC方法，在多任务上取得最优性能并保持训练稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步RL通过解耦rollout生成与优化加速大模型后训练，但训练使用陈旧轨迹，导致行为策略与目标策略不匹配。现有方法主要在actor目标中通过重要性比率控制修正token级策略不匹配，然而优势估计同样继承了行为策略延续带来的误差，即"优势陈旧性"，单靠策略侧修正不足。

Method: 作者推导了通用双通道actor更新的偏差与方差分解，发现策略权重误差与优势估计误差存在不可分离的耦合：其交互产生乘性偏差项，策略权重平方会放大梯度方差中的优势不确定性。据此提出Coupled Off-Policy Correction (COPC)，一种actor-critic方法，结合token级比率掩码与对TD残差的双侧裁剪比率加权，用于回报与优势估计，实现策略侧与优势侧修正的协调。

Result: 在跨陈旧性水平的联合参数扫描中，一个修正参数的效应依赖于另一个参数，甚至可能发生反转，支持了耦合修正假设。COPC在工具集成数学推理与搜索任务上取得最高报告性能，超越各设定下最强的异步基线；具备宽广的高性能参数区域和更好的训练稳定性。在搜索任务中，COPC全程训练稳定，而多数异步基线在训练后期崩溃。这些增益在64步策略陈旧度下依然保持。COPC相比异步PPO仅增加极小的单步时间开销，并相对同步PPO保留1.7倍的步时加速。

Conclusion: 策略侧与优势侧的off-policy修正需要协同设计，COPC通过耦合修正有效缓解优势陈旧性，在性能、稳定性与效率间取得良好平衡，为异步RL后训练提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous RL accelerates large language model post-training by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but trains on stale trajectories. Existing methods primarily correct token-level policy mismatch through importance-ratio control in the actor objective. We show that this \emph{policy-side correction} alone is insufficient: advantage estimates also inherit mismatch from behavior-policy continuations, which we term \emph{advantage staleness}. We derive exact bias and variance decompositions for a general two-channel actor update, revealing nonseparable coupling between policy-weight and advantage-estimation errors: their interaction induces multiplicative bias terms, while squared policy weights amplify advantage uncertainty in gradient variance. This motivates the hypothesis that policy- and advantage-side correction should be coordinated. We introduce Coupled Off-Policy Correction (COPC), an actor--critic method combining token-level ratio masking with two-sided clipped-ratio weighting of TD residuals for return and advantage estimation. Joint parameter sweeps across staleness levels support this hypothesis: the effect of one correction parameter depends on, and can reverse with, the other. COPC achieves the highest reported performance on tool-integrated mathematical reasoning and search, outperforming the strongest reported asynchronous baseline in each setting. It also offers a broad high-performing parameter region and improved training stability. In search, COPC remains stable throughout training, while most evaluated asynchronous baselines collapse late in training. These gains persist at 64-step policy staleness. COPC adds minimal step-time overhead over asynchronous PPO and retains a $1.7\times$ step-time speedup over synchronous PPO.

</details>


### [101] [Lightweight and Versatile Learned Optimization by Recombination of Gradient History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09604)
*Minyoung Choi,Dalta Imam Maulana,Wanyeong Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量化通用学习型优化器，通过按不相交时间跨度平均并动态重组梯度历史，将预测空间压缩为每个梯度均值一个共享标量系数，仅需37k参数、0.87 GPU时训练即可零样本泛化到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型优化器（learned optimizer）通常参数量大、训练成本高，且难以零样本迁移到未见任务；同时长梯度历史带来高昂内存开销，历史贡献难以独立访问。作者希望构建一个轻量、通用、低内存且低FLOPs开销的优化器。

Method: 1) 将梯度历史表示为多个不相交时间跨度上的平均值；2) 对较旧梯度进行渐进式平均以降低长历史的内存成本，同时保持各段贡献独立可访问；3) 把预测空间缩减为每个梯度均值一个标量系数，并由多个参数共享；4) 用一个小型网络（37k参数）学习该系数生成规则。

Result: 在0.87 GPU小时内训练37k参数网络即可零样本泛化到未见任务：BERT-Tiny与GPT-Tiny验证损失分别降低9.1%和0.4%；Vision Transformer上测试精度较Adam提升3.5个百分点，九个图模型上平均提升2.7个百分点；FLOPs开销低至0.3%。

Conclusion: 该优化器以极小参数量、极低训练与计算开销实现了跨架构、跨任务的零样本泛化，验证了"渐进式梯度平均+共享标量系数"设计在通用性与效率上的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight and versatile learned optimizer that dynamically recombines gradient history, represented as averages over disjoint time spans. The optimizer reduces the prediction space to one scalar coefficient per gradient average, shared by multiple parameters. Progressively averaging older gradients minimizes memory cost of long history, while keeping their contributions independently accessible. A 37k-parameter network trained in 0.87 GPU-hours generalizes zero-shot to unseen tasks, lowering validation loss by 9.1% and 0.4% on BERT-Tiny and GPT-Tiny, and improving test accuracy over Adam by 3.5 %p on a Vision Transformer and by 2.7 %p on average across nine graph models, with FLOPs overhead as low as 0.3%.

</details>


### [102] [Decoupled Optimization for Teacher-Student Semi-Supervised Learning via a Pioneer Student](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09609)
*Haorong Han,Jidong Yuan,Chixuan Wei,Yongqi Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现半监督学习的师生框架与联合优化机制分别存在参数耦合和梯度更新不一致两类优化病理，为此提出独立参数空间的辅助分支Pioneer Student (PiS)，周期性回传知识，作为即插即用模块普适提升主流SSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督学习依赖两大核心机制：师生框架下的自训练与有标注/无标注损失的联合优化。作者发现二者各自引入不同的优化病理，限制了模型性能。具体表现为：(1) 参数耦合强制师生严格同步，对学生的强正则化会削弱教师的拟合能力，从而限制可允许的泛化强度；(2) 有标注与无标注损失之间梯度更新一致性失衡，驱动共享参数过早收敛到标注主导的局部极小值，形成全局优化的瓶颈。

Method: 提出 Pioneer Student (PiS)，一个在独立参数空间中运行的辅助分支，周期性将其累积的知识回传至师生（T-S）模型。该设计解耦了师生间的参数耦合，并缓解了标注与无标注损失的梯度更新失衡问题，且可作为即插即用模块集成到现有SSL方法中。

Result: 大量实验表明，PiS 是一个通用的即插即用模块，能够持续提升主流半监督学习方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将辅助分支置于独立参数空间并周期性回传知识，PiS 同时缓解了参数耦合与梯度更新失衡两大优化病理，验证为可普适增强主流SSL方法的即插即用模块。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) relies on two core mechanisms: self-training under the Teacher-Student (T-S) framework and joint optimization of labeled and unlabeled losses. Despite their effectiveness, we find both mechanisms introduce distinct optimization pathologies. First, parameter coupling enforces strict synchronization between teacher and student, where strong regularization on the student degrades the teacher's fitting ability, thereby limiting the permissible generalization intensity. Second, the imbalance in gradient update consistency between labeled and unlabeled losses drives the shared parameters to prematurely converge to labeled-dominated local minima, creating a bottleneck for global optimization. To address both issues, we propose the Pioneer Student (PiS), an auxiliary branch that operates in an independent parameter space and periodically transfers accumulated knowledge back to the T-S model. Extensive experiments show that PiS is a universal plug-and-play module that consistently improves mainstream SSL methods.

</details>


### [103] [Sequential Pretraining Favors Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09611)
*Mohnish Harwani,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义并量化了"首因偏差"（primacy bias）——早期数据分布对后续学习的损害，发现小模型会把学习容量低效地分配给早期分布，而足够过参数化的大模型对此具有鲁棒性，并提出简单正则化方法"暴露疗法"（Exposure Therapy）以改善顺序预训练中的容量分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大神经网络往往能获得小模型学不会的能力，作者想探究这种差距是源于大模型学到了更具代表性的特征，还是源于其对训练中未计入的不利效应更具鲁棒性。为此作者聚焦于"首因偏差"这一不利效应，尤其在基础模型顺序（而非联合）接触异质数据分布的预训练场景中，小模型可能难以学习训练后期出现的分布，从而损害代码、数学、推理等后期强调的能力。

Method: 首先定义并量化首因偏差，即早期数据分布对后续学习的损害程度；通过对比不同规模模型在顺序预训练下的表现来验证容量分配假说；随后提出 Exposure Therapy（ET），一种简单正则化方法，用于促进顺序预训练期间学习容量更高效的分配。

Result: 小模型会将学习容量低效地倾斜于早期分布，而足够过参数化的模型对该效应具有鲁棒性；在顺序预训练中，小基础模型难以学习训练后期遇到的分布。ET 能提升基础模型在后期数据分布上的表现，并在高达十亿参数规模的模型上提升整体能力。

Conclusion: 大基础模型的部分优势可能来自对训练不利效应更强的鲁棒性，而非学到更具代表性的特征；改进训练算法可在较小模型中恢复部分此类优势。

Abstract: Large neural networks often acquire capabilities that small models fail to learn. Does this stem from large models learning more representative features, or from being more robust to unaccounted-for adverse effects introduced during training? We define and quantify one such adverse effect, primacy bias, as the extent to which exposure to early data distributions impairs later learning. We show that small models can allocate learning capacity inefficiently toward early distributions, whereas sufficiently overparameterized models are robust to this effect.
