</details>


### [207] [Who Broke Me? Execution-Guided Repair of Behavioral Dependency Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09267)
*Juntao Chen,Tingting Bi,Yanlin Wang,Patanamon Thongtanunam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 BBCFixer，通过定位“根 API”并过滤库 diff 来为 LLM 智能体提供行为破坏性变更的修复证据，同时发布 BBCBench 基准，实验显示其通过率最高提升 17%、成功修复所需步数最多减少 27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖升级即使不改变库接口也可能引入行为破坏性变更（behavioral breaking changes），这类问题难以修复：失败的测试未必指向真正导致破坏的上游根 API。现有基于 LLM 的修复方法依赖编译器反馈或库文档获取证据，但行为破坏不产生编译错误且常缺乏文档；而无证据的智能体通常也不会自行检索上游证据，导致多数修复失败且未能识别根 API。因此需要一种方法为行为破坏选取有效的上游证据。

Method: 作者提出 BBCFixer：分别在旧版和新版库下运行失败测试，对返回值发生差异的调用进行排序以识别候选根 API，再用该候选 API 过滤库 diff，从而提取与根 API 相关的上游证据供 LLM 智能体修复。同时构建 BBCBench 基准，包含 Python 和 JavaScript 中的 100 个行为依赖破坏案例，用于评估行为破坏修复。

Result: 在 BBCBench 上与两个基线对比：(1) Pure Agent，不接收任何升级证据的智能体；(2) BDUpdater，通过挖掘库文档获取证据的方法。BBCFixer 相比 Pure Agent 平均通过率最高提升 17%，且每次成功修复所需智能体步数最多减少 27%。

Conclusion: BBCFixer 提供了一种为行为破坏定位并选择上游证据的方式，使 LLM 智能体能够更有效、更高效地修复行为破坏性依赖变更；同时 BBCBench 为这类修复研究提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Dependency upgrades can break downstream projects without changing the library interface. Such behavioral breaking changes are difficult for developers to fix, because the failing test does not always point to the root API, the upstream API that causes the break. Existing LLM-based repair methods obtain evidence for the repair from compiler feedback or library documentation. However, a behavioral break produces no compiler feedback and is often undocumented. Agents that receive no upgrade evidence also usually do not retrieve upstream evidence themselves, and most of their failed repairs do not identify the root API.
  The library diff provides useful upstream evidence, but the root API must first be identified to select the relevant part of the diff. We present BBCFixer, a repair method that runs the failing test under the old and new library versions, ranks the calls whose return value differs to identify a candidate root API, and filters the library diff with that candidate. To evaluate repairs of behavioral breaks, we further introduce BBCBench, a benchmark of 100 behavioral dependency breaks in Python and JavaScript.
  We compare BBCFixer on BBCBench with two baselines: (1) Pure Agent, an agent that receives no upgrade evidence, and (2) BDUpdater, a method that mines library documentation. BBCFixer raises the average pass rate by up to 17% relative to Pure Agent and needs up to 27% fewer agent steps per successful repair. BBCFixer thus provides a way to select the upstream evidence behind a behavioral break, so that an LLM agent can repair behavioral breaking changes more effectively and efficiently.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond FAIR: A Fitness Function Framework for Sustainable Research Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09580)
*Irdin Pekaric,Philipp Zech*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文在原有基于FAIR的研究软件可持续性评估框架基础上，引入环境适配函数与安全适配函数，将可持续性从FAIR合规扩展为涵盖FAIR、环境责任与长期安全的多维属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAIR原则虽重要，但未覆盖全部可持续性关切：研究软件还应具备环境责任与长期安全性。过度资源消耗增加环境成本，不安全软件带来维护负担、技术债务及复用障碍，因此需要扩展评估框架。

Method: 在先前提出的可持续研究软件适配函数（fitness function）框架上，新增两组适配函数：环境函数（针对资源效率与执行足迹）和安全函数（针对依赖健康、漏洞暴露与安全配置），用于持续软件评估。

Result: 形成扩展后的适配函数框架，将研究软件可持续性评估由FAIR合规拓展至包含FAIR、环境责任与长期安全的多维度视图。

Conclusion: 研究软件可持续性应被视为包含FAIR、环境责任和长期安全的多维属性，扩展的适配函数框架可支持更全面的持续可持续性评估。

Abstract: Research software sustainability is often assessed through the FAIR principles: findability, accessibility, interoperability and reusability. While important, FAIR does not cover all relevant sustainability concerns. Research software should also be environmentally responsible and secure over time. Excessive resource consumption increases environmental cost, while insecure software creates maintenance overhead, technical debt and barriers to reuse. To this end, in this paper, we extend a previously proposed fitness function framework for sustainable research software beyond FAIR. We introduce two additional sets of fitness functions: environmental functions targeting resource efficiency and execution footprint, and security functions targeting dependency health, vulnerability exposure and secure configuration. Together, these functions broaden continuous software assessment from FAIR compliance to a more complete view of sustainability. The resulting framework treats research software sustainability as a multidimensional property that includes FAIR, environmental responsibility and long-term security.

</details>


### [209] [GRAML: Graph-Grounded Reasoning and Multi-Task Learning for LLM-Based Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09605)
*Ruohan Li,Miaoqing Tian,Haipeng Qu,Ruobing Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GRAML框架，融合图证据、漏洞描述生成与多任务训练以提升LLM的软件漏洞检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件漏洞检测中常因控制流与数据流信息利用不足而性能受限。

Method: 对C/C++程序做静态分析，提取关键源行及带类型行关系作为结构证据；用该证据引导GPT-5执行ToT-VR（树思维引导漏洞推理）生成漏洞描述；再与检测、定位、评估样本合并，构建统一四任务训练数据集。

Result: 在1个分布内测试集与6个分布外数据集上，平均F1为66.67%–68.70%，最高超越SOTA基线30.92%；消融显示ToT-VR与图引导漏洞描述优于标准CoT推理和原始CPG序列化。

Conclusion: 为构建更可靠、更安全的LLM软件工程系统提供实践指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely applied to software vulnerability detection. However, their performance is often limited by insufficient use of control-flow and data-flow information. In this paper, we propose GRAML, a framework that combines graph evidence, vulnerability description generation, and multi-task training. GRAML first performs static analysis on C/C++ programs to extract critical source lines and typed line relations as structural evidence. It then uses this evidence to guide GPT-5 through the Tree-of-Thought-guided Vulnerability Reasoning (ToT-VR) process and generate vulnerability descriptions. These descriptions are further combined with Detection, Localization, and Assessment samples to build a unified four-task training dataset. We evaluate GRAML on an in-distribution (ID) test set and six out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets. The results show that GRAML achieves average F1 scores ranging from 66.67% to 68.70%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by up to 30.92%. Ablation experiments further show that ToT-VR and graph-guided vulnerability descriptions improve detection performance compared with standard Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and raw Code Property Graph (CPG) serializations. These findings provide practical guidance for building more reliable and secure software engineering systems with large language models.

</details>


### [210] [A Chat Assistant for Software Exploration in a 3D Software Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09901)
*Malte Hansen,Karim Issa,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个嵌入在3D软件可视化工具ExplorViz中的聊天助手，基于大语言模型（LLM），支持用户通过自然语言提问当前可视化的软件系统并触发改变可视化的操作。助手通过CopilotKit集成于前端，结合概率性LLM与确定性、基于工具的动作（类似MCP实现），并支持重构可视化中的软件系统。对十一名被试的实验表明，生成的摘要与解释被评价为基本正确，高亮实体、创建新配色主题等操作具有高可用性，但开放式聊天驱动的软件重构效果不一，需更强的LLM护栏与用户反馈机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索性程序理解任务中，用户与3D软件可视化工具的交互存在较高的操作开销。作者希望通过引入基于LLM的聊天助手，以自然语言交互降低这一开销，同时提升用户对软件系统的理解支持。

Method: 构建基于当前LLM的聊天助手，集成于ExplorViz前端（使用CopilotKit库），将概率性LLM与确定性、基于工具的动作相结合（类似MCP的实现方式）。助手可回答关于当前可视化软件系统的问题、触发改变可视化的操作，并支持通过添加、删除或修改软件系统部件来重构可视化。通过包含十一名被试的实证实验，评估感知理解支持与助手触发的工具调用。

Result: 被试普遍认为助手生成的摘要与解释基本正确，并对高亮可视化实体、创建新配色主题等操作的可用性评价较高。相较之下，开放式聊天辅助的软件重构在可视化中的结果喜忧参半，表明需为所用LLM提供更强的护栏（guardrails）并更好地融合用户反馈。总体而言，助手被认为可用且在减少探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销方面具有前景。作者还提供了展示助手使用的视频以及评估所用软件系统的复现包。

Conclusion: 该聊天助手整体被感知为可用且有前景，能够降低探索性程序理解任务中的交互开销；但对于开放式软件重构，需要加强LLM护栏并改进用户反馈的整合。

Abstract: We present a chat assistant for interactive software exploration, embedded in the 3D software visualization tool ExplorViz. The assistant builds upon current Large Language Models (LLMs) and enables users to ask questions about the currently visualized software system and trigger actions that change the visualization through natural language. We integrate the chat assistant in our ExplorViz frontend using the CopilotKit libraries such that probabilistic LLMs are combined with deterministic and tool-based actions similar to implementations using the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The chat assistant is also enabled to restructure the software system by adding, removing, or modifying parts of the software system in the visualization. An empirical experiment with eleven participants evaluated both perceived comprehension support and the tool calls that were triggered by the chat assistant. Participants rated the assistant's generated summaries and explanations as largely correct. They also reported high usability for actions like highlighting entities in the visualization and the creation of new color themes. In contrast, open-ended chat-assisted software restructuring in the visualization showed mixed results. This suggests a need for stronger guardrails for the employed LLM and better incorporation of user feedback. Overall, the assistant was perceived as usable and promising for reducing interaction overhead during exploratory program comprehension tasks. We provide a video presenting the chat assistant's use and a reproduction package of the software system that was used in our evaluation.

</details>


### [211] [Analysis and Visualization of the Linux Kernel's Software Evolution Using the City Metaphor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09910)
*Malte Hansen,Lukas Damerau,Moritz Bugla,David Moreno-Lumbreras,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用软件可视化工具ExplorViz，以3D城市隐喻对Linux内核进行结构与演化可视化分析，并展示Rust文件代码演化的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核代码量超4000万行、维护时间长，理解其内部结构与软件演化极具挑战，亟需有效的可视化手段辅助探索。

Method: 通过自定义分析服务解析Linux Git仓库的提交记录，前端基于Web并采用3D城市隐喻呈现软件结构；为每个文件采集代码行数等指标，并向上累加至目录与提交层级；将指标映射为建筑尺寸或热力图，配合多种可视化、搜索与过滤选项进行交互探索。

Result: 实现了对内核仓库全部文件的可视化，并完成对Linux内核中Rust文件代码演化的可视化分析。

Conclusion: 该方案验证了3D城市隐喻可视化在大规模、长周期开源项目结构与演化分析中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: The Linux kernel is one of the largest and longest-maintained open source projects in existence. With more than 40 million lines of code, understanding the kernel's internal structure and assessing its software evolution is a great challenge. In this paper, we present an approach to visualize the Linux kernel using our software visualization tool ExplorViz. We analyze commits from the Linux Git repository using a custom analysis service. The web-based frontend utilizes the 3D city metaphor for visualization of the software structure. Metrics such as the number of lines are collected for each file and are accumulated for directories and commits. Calculated metrics can be mapped to the building's dimensions or be displayed via a heat map. A wide range of visualization, search, and filter options enable the interactive exploration of the visualized data. We present both a visualization of all files in the kernel repository and a visual analysis of the code evolution of Rust files in the Linux kernel. Demo video: https://youtu.be/3ODnERqD8g0

</details>


### [212] [AgentTracer: Tracing Indirect Prompt Injection Attack through Fine-Grained Intention-Execution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09935)
*Zitong Yao,Jiangrong Wu,Yixi Lin,Anrui Huang,Luoyun Zhang,Yuhong Nan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentTracer，一种意图感知的间接提示注入（IPI）追踪框架，将IPI视为任务意图漂移，通过恢复工具调用间的隐式决策依赖构建意图驱动执行图，实现攻击链重构与注入源定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过调用外部资源完成复杂任务，易受间接提示注入（IPI）攻击，攻击者用恶意指令将智能体重定向到其意图任务。由于IPI在真实环境中难以防御，事后的溯源分析至关重要，用于定位注入源并重构攻击链。但现有追踪方法主要捕捉显式的控制流和数据流依赖，忽视了恶意指令驱动的工具调用之间的隐式关系；这些调用可能缺乏显式依赖并与合法操作交错，导致完整攻击链重构困难。

Method: AgentTracer将IPI视为任务意图漂移，恢复工具调用间的隐式决策依赖，构建"意图驱动执行图"，以任务意图连接分散的工具调用。它结合用户请求与操作知识库构建用户意图授权空间，识别意图漂移的工具调用；从审计中的异常工具调用出发，执行基于目标的剪枝和反向追踪，以重构攻击链并定位注入源与注入点。

Result: 为在有正常任务噪声的场景下评估，作者将AgentDyn和InjecAgent中成功IPI攻击构建的执行日志，与包含1,800个用户请求、9,000个无IPI背景工具调用的正常执行日志相结合。端到端实验中，AgentTracer达到94.17%的注入点准确率和93.56%的路径精确率；对比实验显示，其注入点准确率较现有方法提升18%至54%。

Conclusion: AgentTracer通过意图感知的追踪范式，有效弥补了现有方法忽视隐式依赖的不足，在含背景噪声的执行日志上实现了高精度的注入点定位与攻击链重构，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with external resources to complete complex user tasks, exposing them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), where malicious instructions redirect agents toward attacker-intended tasks. Since IPI is difficult to defend against in real-world environments, post-incident tracing is essential for locating the injection source and reconstructing the attack chain. However, existing tracing methods primarily capture explicit control-flow and data-flow dependencies, overlooking the implicit relationships among tool calls driven by the malicious instruction. These tool calls may lack explicit dependencies and be interleaved with legitimate operations, making complete attack-chain reconstruction difficult. In this paper, we present AgentTracer, an intent-aware tracing framework that treats IPI as task intent drift. AgentTracer recovers implicit decision dependencies among tool calls to construct an Intent-Driven Execution Graph that connects dispersed tool calls by task intent. It combines the user request with an operation knowledge base to construct a user intent authorization space and identify intent-drift tool calls. Starting from an anomalous tool call under audit, AgentTracer performs target-based pruning and backward tracing to reconstruct the attack chain and locate the injection source and injection point. To evaluate AgentTracer in the presence of noise from normal tasks, we combine execution logs constructed from successful IPI attacks in AgentDyn and InjecAgent with normal execution logs containing 1,800 user requests and 9,000 background tool calls without IPI. In end-to-end experiments, AgentTracer achieves 94.17 percent injection-point accuracy and 93.56 percent path precision. Comparative experiments show that AgentTracer improves injection-point accuracy over existing methods by 18 to 54 percent.

</details>


### [213] [Designing Collaborative AI-Driven Workflows for Scientific Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09995)
*Akash Dhruv,Max Knobbe,Pedro Machado,Anshu Dubey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体AI系统用于科学计算代码生成时，应嵌入协作式团队结构而非完全自动化：由领域专家编写规范与计划，智能体在确定性编排模式下编写代码，每阶段都进行数值对比并需人工审核。以Fortran到C++的大型高能物理应用翻译为案例，在14组实验中，带强制限制的简单"作者—审阅者"循环以约为多智能体工作流三分之一的单文件成本，完成了相当数量的文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算中智能体生成的代码需同时满足正确性，并对拥有不同专业背景的团队成员而言可理解，验证难度大，因此不宜完全自动化，而应置于协作团队结构中。

Method: 提出协作式工作流：领域专家负责规范与计划，智能体在确定性编排模式下生成目标代码；每个阶段以与参考代码的数值比较作为验证，并需人工审查批准后方可进入下一阶段。以大型高能物理应用从Fortran到C++的翻译为评测任务，比较不同编排器、设计模式与模型。

Result: 在14组实验中，带强制限制的简单"作者—审阅者"循环所完成的文件数量与多智能体工作流相当，而单文件成本约为后者的三分之一。

Conclusion: 智能体AI在科学计算代码生成中宜采用人机协作、确定性编排与分阶段人工审核的模式；简单的"作者—审阅者"循环在成本效率上优于更复杂的多智能体工作流。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence systems can carry out a broad range of tasks in software engineering and scientific research, from writing and translating code to running workflows for data analysis and visualization. In scientific computing, the difficulty is verifying that agent-generated code is both correct and understandable to teams whose members bring different areas of expertise. We therefore argue that these systems are best used within collaborative team structures rather than as full automation. In the workflows we propose, domain experts write the specification and plan, and agents operate under a deterministic orchestration pattern to write the target code. Each stage ends with a numerical comparison against the reference code and requires human review and approval before the next begins. We evaluate these workflows on the translation of a large high-energy physics application from Fortran to C++, running the same task under different orchestrators, design patterns, and models. Across fourteen experiments, a simple author--reviewer loop with enforced limits completed a comparable number of files to a multi-agent workflow at about one-third of the cost per file.

</details>


### [214] [AdaT$^2$: Adaptive Test Transformations for Black-Box Boundary Testing of Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10141)
*Liting Lin,Boxi Yu,Qinghua Xu,Yuzhong Zhang,Lionel Briand,Emir Muñoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出AdaT²，通过探索性对话抽取策略条件语句，结合测试变换指令与贝叶斯UCB自适应配对选择生成边界测试，在τ³-bench四个领域上有效性和回归检测能力均优于AgentEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的对话智能体必须遵守策略，策略中的每个条件都在用户请求上划分边界，智能体在边界两侧需有不同行为。需要系统性地测试这些边界以确保策略合规，但现有方法（如AgentEval）在生成有效边界测试方面覆盖不足。

Method: AdaT²从LLM扮演用户的探索性对话中抽取智能体回复的陈述，每条陈述描述一个条件及其成立时的预期行为。除单陈述引导的普通测试外，还通过"陈述+测试变换指令"对（如"省略一个必需输入"）生成变换测试，使测试跨越陈述边界或移至另一边界。由于陈述与指令的对数量远超可执行次数且多数不适用，采用自适应配对选择：按新颖性选择陈述，用bandit算法Bayes-UCB选择指令，依据LLM评判器反馈的测试是否产出及智能体是否通过进行学习。基准分别计数每个边界的两侧，并区分由prompt、工具代码或知识库显式定义的边界与LLM从领域知识推断的边界。

Result: 在τ³-bench四个领域上，AdaT²生成的测试中62.7%–83.3%为预期行为显式定义的有效边界测试，每个领域均高于AgentEval（47.7%–68.1%）。变换测试额外覆盖13至46个普通测试遗漏的显式定义边界。作为回归测试，AdaT²测试套件在airline领域检出全部8个植入策略故障、retail领域检出4个；AgentEval测试套件测试数不足其三分之一，分别只检出5个和2个。

Conclusion: AdaT²通过语句抽取、变换指令配对与自适应bandit选择，显著提升了策略边界测试的有效性与覆盖度，并在回归故障检测中展现出更高的检出率与效率。

Abstract: Conversational agents based on large language models (LLMs) must comply with policies. Each condition in a policy draws a boundary between user requests, and the agent must behave differently on its two sides. We present AdaT$^2$, which extracts statements from the agent's replies in exploratory conversations with an LLM acting as the user, and uses the statements to guide boundary test generation. Each statement describes one condition and the behavior expected when the condition holds. Besides plain tests guided by single statements, AdaT$^2$ writes transformed tests guided by pairs of a statement and a test transformation instruction, such as "omit one required input". The instruction of a pair can move a test to the other side of the statement's boundary or to another boundary. The statements and instructions form far more pairs than a run can try, and many pairs are not applicable. Adaptive pair selection therefore chooses the statement of each pair by novelty and the instruction with the bandit algorithm Bayes-UCB, which learns from whether earlier pairs yielded a test and whether the agent passed it according to an LLM judge. Our benchmark counts the two sides of each boundary separately and distinguishes boundaries explicitly defined by the agent's prompt, tool code, or knowledge base from boundaries that the agent's LLM infers from domain knowledge. On four domains of $τ^3$-bench, 62.7% to 83.3% of AdaT$^2$'s tests are valid boundary tests whose expected behavior is explicitly defined, higher in every domain than AgentEval's (47.7% to 68.1%). Transformed tests add 13 to 46 explicitly defined boundaries that plain tests miss. As regression tests, AdaT$^2$'s test suites detect all eight seeded policy faults in the airline domain and four of eight in the retail domain, and AgentEval's test suites, with fewer than a third as many tests, detect five and two, respectively.

</details>


### [215] [Why Software Engineering Is Indispensable in the Age of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10226)
*Alfonso Fuggetta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文论证:具备能力的AI编码代理非但不会使软件工程(SE)与软件工程师过时,反而使其成为不可或缺的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"AI是否会取代软件工程学科与从业者"这一争议,指出若缺失SE这一基础,AI辅助开发将产出貌似合理却不可验证、最终不可信的软件。

Method: 以概念论证为主:识别大语言模型的三大结构性属性——概率性生成、领域无涉(agnosticism)、语义无状态——它们形成无法通过增加训练消除的结构性真空;并提出填补真空所需的四类知识杠杆:方法学知识、领域知识、设计决策与过程决策,并要求将其物化为持久工件。

Result: 论证显示LLM的结构性缺陷恒存,须由软件工程师承担方法学家、协调者与守护者三种角色,通过四类知识杠杆的物化来弥补。

Conclusion: 软件工程与软件工程师在AI时代不是被淘汰的对象,而是使AI辅助开发可信、可验证的必要前提。

Abstract: Can AI make Software Engineering (SE) -- the discipline -- obsolete? And can it make software engineers -- the professionals -- redundant? This paper argues that the rise of capable AI coding agents makes SE and software engineers essential, not obsolete: the missing foundation without which AI-assisted development produces misleadingly plausible, unverifiable, and ultimately untrustworthy software. Three structural properties of large language models (probabilistic generation, agnosticism, and semantic statelessness) create a structural vacuum that no amount of training can eliminate. Filling it requires four knowledge levers: methodological knowledge, domain knowledge, design choices, and process choices. All four must be reified as persistent artifacts, and each requires the software engineer as methodologist, mediator, and custodian.

</details>


### [216] [TestGRAD: Evolving Test Suites via Failure Pattern Momentum for SWE-Agent Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242)
*Pengfei He,Jiayuan Zhou,Shaowei Wang,Ruiqi Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 TestGRAD 框架，将集成补丁选择建模为测试空间优化，通过差分损失、全 CRUD 梯度和失败模式动量三机制，在 SWE-bench Verified 上以 4-agent 集成取得 84.2% Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-agent 集成通过组合不同 agent 的候选补丁提升问题解决率，但关键在于基于测试的补丁选择：需生成测试、执行候选补丁并选出最优补丁。现有测试生成方法作为优化器存在三重局限：缺乏面向集成选择的显式损失；优化方向不完整（多限于新建或删除测试）；一次性生成、不利用反复失败反馈。

Method: 将补丁选择形式化为测试空间优化，即不断演化可执行仓库测试套件直至区分竞争补丁。受带动量梯度下降启发提出 TestGRAD，包含：1）差分损失（Differential loss），以执行结果定义显式目标，使有用测试按行为分离候选补丁；2）全 CRUD 梯度，将更新方向从仅创建/删除扩展为读取现有测试基础设施、新建测试、更新过期断言、仅删除过时测试；3）失败模式动量（Failure Pattern Momentum），从记忆中挖掘高频失败序列，避免重复无效方向并压缩失败历史上下文。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，TestGRAD 配合 4-agent 集成达到 84.2% Pass@1，较最强基线（80.6%）绝对提升 3.6 个百分点，同时将失败历史上下文压缩超过 100 倍。

Conclusion: 将测试生成重构为带显式损失与动量机制的测试空间优化，可有效提升集成补丁选择的判别力与上下文效率，验证了 TestGRAD 在测试优化任务中的有效性。

Abstract: SWE-agent ensembles improve issue resolution by combining candidate patches from different agents with complementary strengths. The central problem is therefore test-based selection: generate tests, execute candidate patches, and identify the best patch. We formulate this process as test-space optimization: evolving an executable repository test suite until it distinguishes competing patches. Existing test-generation methods are limited optimizers. They usually lack an explicit loss for ensemble selection, optimize through incomplete directions that mostly create new tests or delete old ones, and perform one-off generation without feedback from repeated failures. Inspired by gradient descent with momentum, we introduce TestGRAD, a framework for automatic test optimization. TestGRAD centers on three concepts. Differential loss gives the optimizer an explicit execution-defined target: useful tests should separate candidate patches by behavior. Full CRUD gradients expand the update direction from merely creating or deleting tests to reading existing test infrastructure, creating new tests, updating stale assertions, and deleting only obsolete tests. Failure Pattern Momentum mines frequent failure sequences from memory, allowing the optimizer to avoid repeated non-discriminative directions while compressing the failure-history context. On SWE-bench Verified, TestGRAD achieves 84.2% Pass@1 with a 4-agent ensemble, outperforming the strongest baseline (80.6%) by an absolute improvement of 3.6 percentage points, while compressing failure-history context by over $100\times$.

</details>


### [217] [QuSema: Detecting Silent Bugs in Quantum Libraries via Quantum-knowledge-enhanced Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10258)
*Yujin Song,Kaining Zhang,Qixin Zhang,Shuai Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出量子库静默缺陷自主测试智能体 QuSema，以量子语义与文档约束构建源码级语义预言机，在 Qiskit 与 PennyLane 上发现 40 个新缺陷（含 30 个静默缺陷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子库已成为量子算法开发的关键基础设施，但其正确性难以测试。现有技术主要依赖失败型预言机（执行失败或违反运行时检查才暴露问题）或对比型预言机（与其他实现不一致才暴露问题）。当缺乏合适的基于执行的预言机时，这类方法适用性受限，导致部分"静默缺陷"（silent bugs）无法被检出。这些漏检缺陷会产生错误结果，并传播到实验结论、仿真研究和算法设计中，因此需要一种不依赖执行失败或不一致信号的新测试范式。

Method: 提出 QuSema——面向量子库静默缺陷检测的自主测试智能体。其核心思想是以量子语义和文档约束作为源码级语义预言机（source-level semantic oracle），判断实现逻辑是否会对合法输入产生非法输出。工作方式为智能体循环（agentic loop）：反复检视库的 API 文档与源代码，推理量子操作的预期行为，识别潜在的语义偏差，并通过库 API 生成可执行测试加以验证。借助量子领域推理，QuSema 将高层的行为不匹配转化为具体、用户可触发的缺陷报告，从而发现非崩溃类缺陷。作者在 Qiskit 和 PennyLane 上实现了 QuSema。

Result: 在包含 20 个历史静默缺陷的基准上，QuSema 的平均缺陷重定位数（mean bug relocation counts）高于 Claude Code 与 Codex；其中 DeepSeek 配置的成本低于 Claude Code。此外，QuSema 发现了 40 个此前未知的缺陷，均获开发者确认，其中 30 个为静默缺陷。

Conclusion: 以量子语义与文档为语义预言机、结合智能体循环的自主测试方法，能够有效突破传统失败型/对比型预言机的局限，检测出量子库中非崩溃的静默缺陷。QuSema 在历史基准上表现优于通用编码智能体，并具备低成本配置可行性，实际部署中可发现大量此前未知且经开发者确认的缺陷，表明语义级预言机是量子软件测试的有效方向。

Abstract: Quantum libraries are now critical infrastructure for quantum algorithm development, yet their correctness remains difficult to test. Existing testing techniques mainly rely on failure-based or comparison-based oracles, exposing bugs only when executions fail, violate runtime checks, or disagree with another implementation. Their applicability is limited when suitable execution-based oracles are unavailable, leaving some silent bugs undetected. Such missed bugs can produce incorrect results that propagate into experimental conclusions, simulation studies, and algorithmic designs. Here we present QuSema, an autonomous testing agent for finding silent bugs in quantum libraries. QuSema uses constraints from quantum semantics and documentation as a source-level semantic oracle to assess whether implementation logic can produce invalid outputs from valid inputs. It operates through an agentic loop that repeatedly inspects library API documentation and source code, reasons about the intended behavior of quantum operations, identifies potential semantic deviations, and validates them by generating executable tests through library APIs. Guided by quantum-domain reasoning, QuSema turns high-level behavioral mismatches into concrete, user-triggerable bug reports, enabling it to uncover non-crash defects. We implement QuSema for Qiskit and PennyLane. On a benchmark of 20 historical silent bugs, QuSema achieves higher mean bug relocation counts than Claude Code and Codex, with the DeepSeek configuration costing less than Claude Code. QuSema also discovers 40 previously unknown bugs confirmed by the developers, including 30 silent bugs.

</details>


### [218] [Using Small Language Models to Reverse-Engineer Machine Learning Pipelines Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10261)
*Nicolas Lacroix,Frederic Precioso,Mireille Blay-Fornarino,Sebastien Mosser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估小型语言模型（SLM）能否凭借其代码理解与分类能力，从源代码中提取机器学习流水线阶段，以克服现有方法依赖人工标注或分类器对领域多样性支持不足的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML流水线阶段的自动化提取有助于理解实际ML实践，但ML算法与数据的持续演化带来多样性，使既有方法（人工标注不可扩展、分类器难适配领域多样性）表现受限，亟需更可靠的方案。

Method: 采用验证性研究，基于两项代表当前SOTA局限的参考工作：先用Cochran's Q检验比较多个SLM，再用两项McNemar检验评估最优模型相对参考研究的表现；另以Cochran's Q检验考察分类体系（taxonomy）定义变化对性能的影响；最后用拟合优度检验比较SLM分类与既有研究所得ML实践洞察。

Result: 首先，分类体系的措辞显著影响分类性能；其次，表现最佳的SLM结果良好，但未超越其他分类器；第三，三种分类方法得出显著不同的洞察，效应量各异。

Conclusion: 现有分类方法的局限会偏置我们对ML实践的理解；当前SLM无需微调即可取得有前景的结果，但仍存在常见局限，且推理成本高，制约其在大规模研究中的适用性。

Abstract: Context: Once defined a taxonomy of stages structuring Machine Learning (ML) pipelines (e.g. Data Preprocessing, Modeling...), extracting these stages from source code is key for better understanding ML practices. However, the diversity caused by the constant evolution of ML (e.g., algorithms, datasets) makes this task challenging. Existing approaches either rely on non-scalable manual labeling or on classifiers that do not properly support domain's diversity. These limitations call for more reliable solutions.
  Objective: We evaluate whether Small Language Models (SLMs) can leverage their code understanding and classification abilities to address these limitations, and enhance our understanding of practices in ML.
  Method: We conduct a confirmatory study based on two relevant reference works representing current limitations in the state-of-the-art. We first compare several SLMs using Cochran's Q test, then evaluate the best-performing model against reference studies via two McNemar's tests. An additional Cochran's Q test examines how taxonomy definition variations affect the SLM performance. Finally, goodness-of-fit tests compare ML practice insights from SLM classification with those from prior studies.
  Results: First, we found that the taxonomy wording significantly impacts classification performance. Second, the best performing SLM yielded good results, yet, without outperforming other classifiers. Third, the three classification methods led to significantly different insights, with varying effect sizes, when exploring practices of data scientists.
  Conclusions: Limitations of existing classification methods bias our understanding of ML practices. While current SLMs show promising results without prior fine-tuning, they still exhibit common limitations, in addition to inference high costs challenging their applicability in large-scale studies.

</details>


### [219] [When Sub-Agents Work in Parallel: The Promises and Pitfalls of Dynamic Concurrency in Long-Horizon Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10263)
*Han Li,HanHaoNing Li,Ziqian Jiang,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文把"动态并发"作为一种执行策略，在354个任务、2124次执行上对Codex、Claude Code与Kimi Code进行开启/关闭并发的受控配对比较，系统刻画其端到端效果、调度行为及失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从有界软件工程任务走向长时程开发，动态并发成为扩展复杂任务的潜在手段。但已有研究多关注短任务上的失败或预定义多智能体工作流中的协作，缺乏对前沿智能体在不同任务复杂度下动态并发表现的洞察。

Method: 将动态并发视为一种执行策略，对Codex、Claude Code和Kimi Code在策略启用与禁用条件下进行受控配对比较；覆盖354个任务、2124次执行，跨越不同任务复杂度与执行时程，评估端到端效果与调度行为，并对配对轨迹进行分析。

Result: 识别出13种并发特有失败模式、28种可观察模式，并刻画了动态并发带来优势的条件；同时揭示短任务中模型能力主导结果，而长时程开发中编排成为任务完成的核心。

Conclusion: 动态并发是长时程编码任务中关键的编排机制，其收益依赖于任务复杂度与执行时程等条件；研究为前沿编码智能体的并发策略设计与失败诊断提供了实证基础。

Abstract: As coding agents advance from bounded software engineering tasks toward long horizon development, dynamic concurrency offers a promising way to scale complex development tasks. Under this policy, agents decide during execution whether and how to spawn concurrent sub-agents. Model capability largely determines outcomes on shorter tasks, whereas long horizon development makes orchestration central to task completion. Existing work, focused on coding agent failures on shorter tasks or collaboration in predefined multiagent workflows, offers little insight into dynamic concurrency in frontier agents across task complexity. We study dynamic concurrency as an execution policy through controlled comparisons of matched Codex, Claude Code, and Kimi Code executions with the policy enabled or disabled. Across 354 tasks and 2,124 executions spanning a range of task complexities and execution horizons, we evaluate its end to end effects and scheduling behavior, and analyze matched trajectories to characterize 13 concurrency specific failure modes, 28 observable patterns, and the conditions under which it provides an advantage.

</details>


### [220] [TaoD2C-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Industrial UI Code Generation Beyond Visual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10374)
*Chengwei Shi,Yunnong Chen,Tingting Zhou,Qiang Lu,Shiyu Yue,Xinyuan Hu,Jianfang Ru,Liuqing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TaoD2C-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大模型在工业级设计到代码（D2C）任务中，生成满足实现需求约束的UI代码的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的关键挑战在于从视觉识别迈向约束感知的跨模态推理。工业设计到代码（D2C）场景尤为典型：需将UI设计图与杂乱的图层元数据结合，推断组件与布局的实现需求，并在目标库约束下用代码实现，但现有研究缺乏对真实工业场景下该能力的充分评估。

Method: 构建TaoD2C基准。数据集包含来自17个商业平台的2,861个生产设计，由专家标注97,652条，覆盖Component、Group、Alignment、Position四类，区分"必需约束"与"可选实现方式"。基准定义三项任务：端到端UI代码生成、需求推断、需求实现。并据此评测八个多模态大模型。

Result: 评测八个MLLM显示，在生成满足实现需求的UI代码方面存在显著差距，且在需求推断与需求实现上表现出不同的性能特征。进一步表明，模型的视觉重建能力并不必然意味着其能生成满足实现需求的代码。

Conclusion: TaoD2C揭示了当前MLLM在工业级UI代码生成中约束感知推理能力的不足，且视觉重建与代码实现需求的能力并不等价。研究公开TaoD2C以推动工业UI代码生成的相关研究。

Abstract: A key challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is moving beyond visual recognition to constraint-aware cross-modal reasoning. This involves combining visual cues with information from other modalities to understand elements' relationships under domain-specific rules. This challenge is acutely evident in industrial design-to-code (D2C), which converts user interface (UI) designs into code and requires MLLMs to connect design images with disorganized layer metadata, infer component and layout implementation requirements, and realize them in code under target-library constraints. However, these capabilities remain insufficiently evaluated in realistic industrial settings. To fill this gap, we present TaoD2C-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs' ability to generate UI code that satisfies implementation requirements in industrial applications. The TaoD2C dataset consists of 2,861 production designs from 17 commercial platforms with 97,652 expert annotations across four categories: Component, Group, Alignment, and Position. These annotations distinguish required constraints from permitted implementation choices. TaoD2C-Bench defines three tasks: end-to-end UI code generation, requirement inference, and requirement realization. Evaluating eight MLLMs reveals substantial gaps in generating UI code that satisfies implementation requirements, alongside distinct performance profiles in inference and realization. We further show that MLLMs' visual reconstruction ability does not necessarily imply an ability to generate code that meets these requirements. We release TaoD2C to support research on industrial UI code generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Trustworthy Domain-Specific AI for Structured Knowledge Retrieval and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08894)
*Ryan C. Barron*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可扩展架构，将非结构化领域文本转化为结构化知识以支持检索与推理，整合半自动语料整理、语义结构化、检索与推理，并引入Binary Bleed、HNMFk、T-SRAG及张量链接预测等模块，在多个领域验证了检索精度与幻觉缓解等效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非结构化、领域特定文本难以直接用于检索与推理的问题，作者希望构建一个可解释、可部署、模块化的流水线，将此类文本转化为结构化知识，并提升检索的语义保真度、减少幻觉、支持推理。

Method: 提出集成半自动语料整理、语义结构化、检索与推理的可解释流水线。核心方法包括：Binary Bleed（二进制搜索适配方法，降低NMF低秩搜索复杂度）；HNMFk（带自动潜在特征选择的层次非负矩阵分解，在专家指导下生成可解释主题分类体系）。这些表示填充类型化知识图谱与语义对齐向量库，并通过事件驱动基底同步。T-SRAG动态路由查询跨检索路径；对比对齐将文档与查询嵌入映射到层次主题结构；基于张量的链接预测识别并补全知识图谱缺失链接。

Result: 在网络安全、法律、材料科学与医疗等应用中展示了检索精度提升、早期趋势检测、假设生成以及幻觉缓解方面的改进。

Conclusion: 该论文为可信任、领域特定的AI系统提供了一个可部署、模块化的基础，使其能够对结构化知识进行检索与推理。

Abstract: This dissertation presents a scalable architecture for transforming unstructured, domain-specific text into structured knowledge for retrieval and reasoning. It integrates semi-automatic corpus curation, semantic structuring, retrieval, and inference into an interpretable pipeline.
  The research introduces Binary Bleed, an adapted binary search method that reduces low-rank search complexity for Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF), and Hierarchical NMF with automatic latent feature selection (HNMFk), a depth-adaptive topic modeling method that produces interpretable taxonomies guided by subject matter experts. These representations populate a typed Knowledge Graph and a semantically aligned Vector Store containing extracted latent features, synchronized through an event-driven substrate.
  Tensor-Structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (T-SRAG) dynamically routes queries across retrieval paths. Contrastive alignment maps document and query embeddings to hierarchical topic structures to improve semantic fidelity and reduce hallucinations. Beyond retrieval, tensor-based link prediction identifies and completes missing links in the Knowledge Graph, supporting inference grounded in citation structure.
  Applications across cybersecurity, law, materials science, and healthcare demonstrate improvements in retrieval precision, early trend detection, hypothesis generation, and hallucination mitigation. The dissertation provides a deployable, modular foundation for trustworthy, domain-specific AI systems that retrieve and reason over structured knowledge.

</details>


### [222] [What Transfers from a VLM Teacher? Comparing Supervision Signals for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09177)
*Saba Sturua,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究对比学习式视觉文档检索器中VLM教师信号的用法：将教师从"增强正样本"转向"评判挖掘出的负样本候选"，在固定学生、数据、优化器和评测的条件下取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比训练中每个查询仅有一个标注相关页（正样本），负样本则被默认为不相关；而近期方法多把VLM教师用于增强正样本（注意力迁移或描述对齐）。作者质疑：标签对负样本候选没有任何信息，教师是否更应花在负样本侧来评判这些候选。

Method: 在固定学生、数据、优化器与评测的前提下，用VLM教师对检索器挖掘出的负样本候选进行评判（teacher-judged hard negatives）并做分数蒸馏；对比描述对齐与注意力接地两种正样本侧用法；并与无教师的规则式筛选（从同一挖掘池中以相同训练算力选4个候选，包括现有系统使用的正样本感知阈值）进行对照；同时开展标注者审计与教师评分分布分析。

Result: 教师评判的难负样本与分数蒸馏将ViDoRe v2的nDCG@5从55.2提升至62.6和63.0；描述对齐仅增益2.6分，注意力接地无可测增益。相比无教师规则（最佳者为当前系统使用的正样本感知阈值），教师判断在v2上额外加4.1分、v3上加1.7分。标注者认为查询前四挖掘候选中约两个实际相关但均未被标注，说明标签不完整、训练把相关页当作负样本推开。教师信号较粗糙：在贪心解码的0-100评分提示下82%评分落在量表两端，仅用相关/不相关二分仍保留大部分蒸馏增益。十人审计显示教师处于人类标注者变异范围内，且在查询有唯一确定答案时可靠。

Conclusion: 在对比式视觉文档检索中，VLM教师用于评判负样本候选比用于增强正样本更有效，收益源于标签的不完整性；教师判断在难负样本选择上优于无教师规则，且与人类标注者的一致性和变异范围相当。作者开源代码、330万条教师判断与页面描述、挖掘池、人工审计及训练适配器。

Abstract: Visual document retrievers are trained contrastively: each query is matched to one page labelled relevant - the positive - and pushed away from negatives, pages presumed irrelevant. Recent methods distil a vision-language model (VLM) teacher into the retriever by enriching that positive, transferring the teacher's attention over it or a description of it. We ask whether the teacher is better spent on the other side, judging the candidates the retriever mines as negatives, which the label says nothing about. With student, data, optimizer and evaluation fixed, teacher-judged hard negatives and score distillation raise ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5 from 55.2 to 62.6 and 63.0; description alignment, as adapted here, gains 2.6 points and attention grounding nothing measurable. Against teacher-free rules that select four candidates from the same mined pool at identical training compute, the best of which is the positive-aware threshold current systems use, the teacher's judgement adds 4.1 points on v2 and 1.7 on v3. This is consistent with how incomplete the labels are. Annotators judge about two of a query's four top-ranked mined candidates relevant, none of them labelled, so training pushes the retriever away from relevant pages treated as negatives. What reaches the student is coarse: under a greedily decoded 0-100 rating prompt, 82% of the teacher's ratings come back at one end of the scale or the other, and a relevant/irrelevant partition keeps most of the distillation gain. A ten-annotator audit places the teacher within the range of variation among human annotators, and finds it reliable where a query has a single determinate answer. We release the code, the teacher's 3.3M judgements and page descriptions, the mined pools, the human audit and the trained adapters at https://github.com/elastic/vdr-teacher-signals.

</details>


### [223] [Quantize by Drift: Label-Free Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Text Embedders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09227)
*Hyojung Han,Jongmin Kim,Seung-Hun Jeon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出以"量化引起的表征漂移"作为无标签的逐模块敏感性信号，用于文本嵌入器的混合精度训练后量化分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化需要逐模块敏感性信号，但文本嵌入器最直观的指标（模块量化对检索质量的损害）依赖实际部署中通常不具备的相关性标注，因此需要一种免标注、免搜索的替代信号。

Method: 量化单个模块后重新编码语料，记录输出嵌入相对全精度位置的偏移量，即量化引起的表征漂移，并以其作为配置级排序信号，在硬性打包字节预算下做一次加性分配，无需标签与搜索；在五个开发用嵌入器上评测，并与LieQ基线、均匀精度及迁移的符号几何自适应进行对比，其中三个嵌入器在方法与假设冻结封存后才首次使用。

Result: 配置级漂移对留出检索质量的排序达到宏平均Spearman 0.911；敏感性可跨校准语料与检索域迁移；模块漂移可排序一致地复合但数值上不可加；基于相关性得到的敏感度无一致增益。预注册的方向性假设在主预算下相对原LieQ标准成立（3/3，无崩溃），漂移得分在2/3情形下超过双侧LieQ钢人基线；但主预算下漂移在三个嵌入器上数值均低于同预算均匀精度（-0.99、-0.85、-1.01分）。

Conclusion: 输出漂移是一种稳健的粗粒度敏感性信号，而非普遍最优的分配目标：它避免了迁移式符号几何自适应的灾难性失效，并可在均匀精度崩溃的紧张预算下保持可用，但围绕强均匀工作点的细粒度重分配问题仍未解决。

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization needs a per-module sensitivity signal; for a text embedder the obvious one -- the retrieval quality a module costs when quantized -- needs relevance labels that deployments rarely have. We measure a label-free substitute: quantization-induced representation drift, obtained by quantizing one module, re-encoding the corpus, and recording how far the output embeddings moved from their full-precision positions. What is specific is the observable: the deployed output representation a dense retriever ranks with. Across five development embedders, configuration-level drift orders sampled mixed-precision plans against held-out retrieval quality at a macro Spearman of 0.911, the sensitivity transports across calibration corpora and retrieval domains in the usable regime, module drifts compose rank-consistently but not numerically, and relevance-derived sensitivity adds no consistent value. The method is one additive allocation under a hard packed-byte budget, with no labels and no search. On three embedders held untouched until method, baselines and hypotheses were frozen and sealed, the pre-registered directional hypothesis against the prior LieQ criterion holds (3/3 at the main budget, no collapse) and drift scores above a two-sided LieQ steelman in 2/3; but at the main budget drift is numerically lower than same-budget uniform precision on all three (-0.99, -0.85, -1.01 points), having reduced module and whole-model drift as designed. Output drift is thus a robust coarse sensitivity signal, not a universally optimal allocation objective: it avoids the catastrophic failures of the transferred signed-geometry adaptation and can remain usable at stressed budgets where uniform collapses, but fine-grained redistribution around a strong uniform operating point remains unresolved.

</details>


### [224] [Reading Position Is the Baseline to Beat: A Time-Ordered Evaluation of Personalised Highlight Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09262)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文质疑“向读者推荐相似读者高亮”的默认方案，提出应以阅读位置为基线，并强调个性化评估须按时间顺序进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读产品的首要高亮是最廉价的个人信号，常规做法是推荐相似早期读者所标注内容并以流行度衡量效果。作者认为真正的基线应是阅读位置，而非流行度或相似度方法。

Method: 在单一社交高亮平台上做时间顺序评估，使用7,343个读者-页面配对（1,511个页面，且已有一次高亮）。方法包括：仅用位置对首个高亮之后的句子排序；与流行度及两种相似度方法对比；并考察按距最近高亮距离折扣的流行度；还通过合成读者与乱序评估进行对照检验。

Result: 仅凭位置排序（不使用其他读者数据）在预测下一次高亮进入前五的比例为47%，流行度为26%，两种相似度方法中较优者为29%。但该基线依赖目标：在所有后续高亮上位置法不敌流行度；而按距最近高亮距离折扣的流行度在合适尺度下，在两类目标上均优于流行度与两种相似度方法。预先指定的比较中，两种相似度方法在所有后续高亮上的平均精度均未超过流行度，且可排除+0.01的增益。

Conclusion: 相似度方法的增益不足以证明其捕捉到读者偏好：合成读者共享偏好时会自然产生增益，乱序评估也会显示方法只是跟随读者所在位置。位置结果属探索性、未经确认。文档内个性化评估应按时间顺序并对照阅读位置进行。

Abstract: A reader's first highlights on a page are the cheapest personal signal a reading product has. The natural plan is to suggest what similar earlier readers marked, and to judge the result against popularity. We argue that the baseline to beat is reading position. In a time-ordered evaluation on one social highlighting platform (7,343 reader-page pairs on 1,511 pages after one highlight), ranking the sentences just below a reader's first highlight, with no other reader's data, puts the next highlight in the top five 47% of the time, against 26% for popularity and 29% for the better of two similarity methods. The baseline depends on the target: over all later highlights that ranking loses to popularity, while popularity discounted by distance from the latest highlight, at the scale with the best average precision of three tried, beats popularity and both similarity methods on both targets. In a comparison specified in advance, neither similarity method shows a gain over popularity in average precision over all later highlights, from one to five highlights, and a gain of +0.01 is excluded. Nor would a gain by itself show that a method has found a reader's preferences: synthetic readers who share one set of preferences produce one, and an evaluation out of time order shows a method where the reader went. The position results are exploratory and unconfirmed. Personalisation inside a document should be evaluated in time order and against reading position.

</details>


### [225] [From Chunks to Functional Evidence: Function-Aware Retrieval for EDA Documentation QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09361)
*Xiaotian Qiu,Kairui Liu,Shi Chenyi,Jinyuan Deng,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向EDA文档的函数感知RAG检索单元：将类型化构件组织为超边表示的功能单元，训练编码器对齐查询与功能单元，并结合单元检索与分块检索，经统一重排序后供给生成器。在EDADocEval-QA上ROUGE-L较Chunk RAG提升37.1%、较最强图基线提升55.6%；在ORD-MMBench上较最强基线提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂技术文档场景下，RAG瓶颈不在模型推理，而在于查询与知识组织方式之间的不匹配。EDA文档中回答所需信息分散于异构但紧耦合的构件中，传统孤立分块或二元关系难以覆盖。

Method: 重新设计RAG的基本检索单元：将类型化构件聚合为EDA功能单元，每个单元以超边（hyperedge）记录并链接至源分块；训练编码器实现查询与功能单元的对齐；将单元检索与直接分块检索相结合；把选中的单元映射回其源分块后，由统一重排序器选择交给生成器的证据。

Result: 在新建的EDADocEval-QA数据集上，ROUGE-L较Chunk RAG提升37.1%，较最强图基线提升55.6%；在公开基准ORD-MMBench上，ROUGE-L较最强基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持在所评估的EDA文档设置中采用函数感知的证据组织方式。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to ground answers in documents. For complex technical documentation, however, the primary bottleneck is often not model reasoning but a mismatch between a query and the way knowledge is organized for retrieval. This mismatch is pronounced in Electronic Design Automation (EDA) documentation, where the information needed for an answer is scattered across heterogeneous yet tightly coupled artifacts. We therefore redesign the basic retrieval unit of RAG. Instead of operating on isolated chunks or binary relations, we collect typed artifacts into EDA functional units. Each unit is recorded as a hyperedge with links to its source chunks. We then train an encoder to align queries with functional units and combine unit retrieval with direct chunk retrieval. After mapping the selected units back to their sources, a unified reranker chooses the evidence given to the generator. On the newly constructed EDADocEval-QA dataset, our method improves ROUGE-L by 37.1% over Chunk RAG and 55.6% over the strongest graph baseline. On the public ORD-MMBench benchmark, it improves ROUGE-L by 30.0% over the strongest baseline. These results support function-aware evidence organization in the evaluated EDA documentation settings.

</details>


### [226] [Finding the Right Balance: Relevance and Diversity in LLM Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09412)
*Guillaume Brouillette,Faustin Kagabo,Usef Faghihi,Nadia Ghazzali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 检索多样化在RAG中效果取决于候选池冗余度与查询所需证据数量：干净池上有害，多证据且高冗余时有益；据此提出基于有效去重文档数的查询自适应多样化规则，可从现有嵌入计算并跨数据集/编码器迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究对检索多样化是否提升检索与答案质量结论不一致，缺乏对其适用条件的统一解释，因此需要厘清多样化收益随何种因素变化，并给出可操作的使用准则。

Method: 通过受控近重复注入与生产式重叠分块构造不同冗余水平的候选池；分析多样化对相关性、证据覆盖与答案质量的影响，并考察查询所需不同证据数量（单证据/多证据）的作用。提出查询自适应规则：仅当最近邻top-k选择中的有效去重文档数低于查询的证据需求时才进行多样化；该规则由现有嵌入计算。另提出RNG-Score几何重排器，带精确最近邻回退，其间隔指示重复结构。

Result: 在干净池上，多样化损害相关性、证据覆盖与答案质量；在多证据任务中，当冗余导致最近邻检索反复选中重复段落时，多样化转为有益。所提规则捕获了大部分可获得的收益，可跨数据集与编码器迁移，并对单证据查询自动退化为最近邻检索。RNG-Score的间隔可反映重复结构。

Conclusion: 检索多样化应基于可观测的冗余度与查询证据需求进行选择性使用，而非普遍启用。

Abstract: Retrieval diversification is widely available in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frameworks, yet prior studies disagree on whether it improves retrieval and answer quality. We show that its effectiveness varies primarily with candidate-pool redundancy, in a pattern consistent with the number of distinct evidence pieces a query requires. Using controlled near-duplicate injection and production-style overlapping chunking, we find that diversification harms relevance, evidence coverage and answer quality on clean pools, but becomes beneficial on multi-evidence tasks when redundancy causes nearest-neighbor retrieval to select repeated passages. We therefore introduce a query-adaptive rule that diversifies only when the effective number of distinct documents in the nearest-neighbor top-$k$ selection falls below the query's evidence requirement. Computed from existing embeddings, the rule captures most of the achievable gain, transfers across datasets and encoders and automatically reduces to nearest-neighbor retrieval for single-evidence queries. We also introduce RNG-Score, a geometric reranker with an exact nearest-neighbor fallback whose margin indicates duplicate structure. Overall, we conclude that diversification should be used selectively, based on observable redundancy and evidence requirements.

</details>


### [227] [Towards Explaining Query Expansion Performance in Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09724)
*Sourav Saha,Aditya Dutta,Soumajit Pramanik,Mandar Mitra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文从"理想扩展查询(IEQ)"和"可分性"两个互补视角解释查询扩展(QE)方法在不同查询上性能差异的原因，并提出可分性度量及IEQ近似方法，在TREC多个集合上的实验验证了越接近IEQ、可分性越高的扩展查询检索效果越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QE长期用于缓解IR中的词汇不匹配问题，且在基于LLM的现代检索系统中仍具价值，但没有任何单一QE方法能在所有查询上稳定优于其他方法，其性能差异的成因尚缺乏解释，因此需要从理论上刻画并解释这种性能波动。

Method: 提出两个互补视角：一是理想扩展查询(IEQ)，即在给定下游BM25检索模型下能使检索效果最大化的假想查询；二是可分性视角，用Cohen's d量化给定扩展查询下相关文档与非相关文档得分的区分程度。据此设计可分性度量并提出近似IEQ的实际公式，进而分析这些因素与检索效果之间的关系。

Result: 在TREC Robust、TREC DL 2019-2022 passage集合及TREC DL 2019-2020 document集合上的大量实验显示若干规律：越接近理想扩展查询的扩展查询，其检索效果通常越高；相关与非相关文档的可分性为理解QE性能提供了互补性视角。

Conclusion: IEQ接近度与可分性可共同解释QE方法间的性能差异，为查询扩展的效果波动提供了可量化的理论解释框架，并提示可分性可作为QE性能的补充性分析指标。

Abstract: Query Expansion (QE) techniques have long been widely used in Information Retrieval (IR) to address the vocabulary mismatch problem. They remain relevant in modern retrieval systems, including those based on large language models (LLMs). However, no single QE method consistently outperforms others across all queries. This work seeks to explain the variation in QE performance through two complementary perspectives. The first is the concept of an Ideal Expanded Query (IEQ)--a hypothetical query that maximizes retrieval effectiveness with a downstream BM25 retrieval model. The second is a separability perspective, which quantifies how distinctly relevant and non-relevant documents are scored for a given expanded query using Cohen's (d). We develop a separability measure and practical formulations to approximate the IEQ and investigate how these factors relate to retrieval effectiveness. Extensive experiments on the TREC Robust collection, TREC DL 2019-2022 passage collections, and TREC DL 2019-2020 document collections reveal several interesting patterns. In particular, we find that expanded queries that are closer to the ideal expanded query tend to achieve higher retrieval effectiveness. We further show that the separability of relevant and non-relevant documents provides a complementary perspective for understanding QE performance.

</details>


### [228] [The Impact of Backbone Evolution on LLM-Based Relevance Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09820)
*Chuting Yu,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了LLM相关性判断在模型版本演进下的行为，发现模型能力提升并不必然带来相关性判断质量的提升或稳定性，固定提示词在同一模型家族跨版本迁移时可能出现性能回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM快速迭代，学界普遍假设在相同的相关性判断提示词下，更强大的新版本模型会与人工判断达成更高一致性。作者质疑这一假设，希望检验基于LLM的相关性判断器在骨干模型演进过程中是否真的保持稳定与改进。

Method: 固定提示词，选取代表性的单提示词方法（UMBRELA）和基于评分标准（rubric-based）的提示方法（EXAM），在商业模型（Gemini、GPT）与开源权重模型（Qwen、Llama）的连续版本上逐一评估其相关性判断表现，并分析回退现象的潜在驱动因素。

Result: 总体上没有一致证据表明更新版本会带来更好的相关性判断器；即使聚合性能持平或提升，也不能说明判断具有稳定性——早期版本做出的正确判断在后续版本中未必被保留；作者进一步探究了这些回退现象的潜在原因。

Conclusion: 不应假设为某一骨干版本设计并验证过的判断提示词，在模型更新后仍能表现相当或更好，即便是在同一模型家族内部；LLM相关性判断器的有效性需针对具体模型版本重新验证。

Abstract: LLMs are evolving rapidly, with newer models offering stronger capabilities. This suggests that in LLM-based relevance judging, more capable models will achieve higher agreement with human judgements under the same prompt. We challenge this understanding by investigating the behavior of LLM-based relevance judges under backbone evolution. Keeping the prompts fixed, we evaluate a representative single-prompt (UMBRELA) and a rubric-based prompt (EXAM) across sequential model versions of commercial (Gemini, GPT) and open-weight (Qwen, Llama) models. Overall, we find no consistent evidence that newer versions lead to better relevance judges. Crucially, similar or improved aggregate performance does not imply judgment stability: correct judgements made by an earlier version of an LLM backbone are not necessarily preserved by later versions. We investigate the potential drivers of these regressions. Our findings caution against the assumption that judging prompts designed and validated for one backbone version will perform equivalently or better when the model is updated, even within the same family.

</details>


### [229] [Inverting Multi-Vector Visual Document Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09920)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了多向量视觉文档检索器的一个安全漏洞：攻击者仅凭存储在第三方向量库中的索引（约千个patch向量/页）即可反向重建原始页面图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量视觉文档检索器将每页存储为约千个patch向量，常托管于第三方向量数据库。由于无法从向量直接读取页面，该索引通常被视为比原页面更不敏感。但索引保留光栅顺序的逐patch向量，且每个向量由预训练用于阅读文档的视觉语言模型计算，作者据此假设：掌控或入侵该索引者可仅凭索引还原页面内容，从而构成隐私泄露风险。

Method: 将索引反演建模为条件文档图像生成任务，从向量中推断攻击所需信息（编码器、页面形状，以及乱序向量的排序）。在ViDoRe v3基准上实验：以原始索引反演重建页面；用重建页面作为查询检索原存储索引；测试两种低成本防护（token池化与乱序）；并训练模型恢复乱序索引的顺序；最后将同一攻击零改动迁移到另一多向量检索器以验证泛化性。

Result: ViDoRe v3上，从原始索引反演可恢复47%的单词和45%的敏感token；重建页面作为查询时98.4%可将源页面排第一。token池化与乱序两种防护均将单词召回降至约8%。恢复乱序顺序的模型将源页面排第一比例从3.8%提升至93.5%，而反演池化索引仍属开放难题。迁移实验中，同一攻击使另一多向量检索器的反演页面仍有70.2%将源页面排第一，单词召回低于最近邻基线。

Conclusion: 多向量视觉文档检索器通过其存储索引面临反演攻击脆弱性，索引应视同其所编码的文档一并受到安全保护。

Abstract: Prevailing multi-vector visual document retrievers store each page as about a thousand patch vectors, often in vector databases run by a third party. Since no one can read a page from its vectors, this index is easily treated as less sensitive than the page. However, because the index keeps one vector per patch in raster order, and each vector is computed by a vision-language model pre-trained to read documents, we hypothesize that whoever runs or breaches the store can reproduce a page from its index alone. We frame inversion as conditional document image generation and infer from the vectors what the attack needs: the encoder, the page shape and, for shuffled vectors, their order. On the ViDoRe v3 benchmark, pages inverted from raw indices recover 47% of the words and 45% of the sensitive tokens. Used as queries against the stored indices, they rank their source page first 98.4% of the time. We test two cheap protections, token pooling and shuffling, which both cut word recall to about 8%. A model that restores the order of a shuffled index raises the share of source pages ranked first from 3.8% to 93.5%, while inverting a pooled index remains open. To test generalisation, we apply the same attack unchanged to another multi-vector retriever: its inverted pages still rank their source page first 70.2% of the time, though its word recall stays below a nearest-neighbour baseline. Multi-vector visual document retrievers are therefore vulnerable to inversion through their stored index, which should be protected like the documents it encodes.

</details>


### [230] [Training with Missed Targets in Generative Recommendation: Separating Supervision from Probability Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10124)
*Xuesi Wang,Yangbin Shi,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将生成式推荐器遗漏的目标追加到重排训练列表，会同时改变三件事：已检索目标的权重、对追加目标的监督、以及两组目标对概率的竞争。论文用三个匹配损失分离这些效应，发现竞争成分会损害返回项排序，应按生成器逐一评估是否采用该训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器只返回有限候选集，可能遗漏观测到的目标。常见做法是把这些遗漏目标追加到重排器训练列表中，但推理时仍只对原始候选排序。这一操作同时引入三种变化（检索目标权重、追加目标监督、两组竞争概率），因此简单的"追加/不追加"对比无法解释返回项排序的变化，需要拆解各成分的独立效应。

Method: 构造三个匹配的损失函数：固定检索目标权重，分别单独引入追加目标监督与组间竞争。其中间损失在两个组内分别训练但各自独立归一化，从而避免仅用于训练的目标与推理候选发生概率竞争。实验使用公开的 OneRec 模型与本地训练的 Amazon 生成器进行验证。

Result: 组间竞争会损害返回项排序。在四个预设的 Amazon Video Games 对比中，去除竞争使全目标归一化折损累积增益（FT-NDCG）提升 7.8%–22.2%；跨用户的 95% 区间以及四个中的三个跨训练运行的区间均排除零。保守的开发集规则在某一留出类别中为三个生成器中的两个选择了追加目标训练，在另一类别中则拒绝了全部三个，从而避免了 1.7% 的损失。

Conclusion: 候选补全（candidate completion）应针对每个生成器分别评估，而不应自动套用。其收益或损害主要取决于组间竞争效应，需通过匹配损失实验单独识别后再决定是否采用。

Abstract: Generative recommenders return a limited candidate set and may omit observed targets before reranking. A training strategy appends these missed targets to reranker training lists, although inference still ranks only original candidates. This operation simultaneously changes retrieved-target weight, adds supervision over appended targets, and makes the two groups compete for probability. An append/no-append comparison therefore cannot explain changes in returned-item rankings. We construct three matched losses that hold retrieved-target weight fixed while introducing appended-target supervision and group competition separately. The intermediate loss trains within both groups but normalizes them separately, preventing training-only targets from competing with inference candidates. Experiments with a released OneRec model and locally trained Amazon generators show that this competition can harm returned-item ranking. In four prespecified Amazon Video Games comparisons, removing it improved full-target normalized discounted cumulative gain (FT-NDCG) by 7.8--22.2\%; 95\% intervals over users and three of four intervals over training runs excluded zero. A conservative development-set rule selected appended-target training for two of three generators in one held-out category and rejected it for all three in another, avoiding a 1.7\% loss. Candidate completion should therefore be evaluated for each generator rather than applied automatically.

</details>


### [231] [Does Document Structure Help Dense Retrieval? A Placebo-Controlled Ablation of Four Mechanisms Across Two Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10170)
*Andrey Kuehlkamp,Priscila Correa Saboia Moreira,Samuel Rund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次在统一协议下对检索增强生成中的四种文档结构处理进行机制隔离消融，并引入语义零效果的安慰剂对照，发现结构对齐分块与真实标题路径的增益来自内容而非token扰动，而朴素两阶段层次检索反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别在不同语料、嵌入模型和指标上支持结构对齐分块、LLM生成块上下文、标题路径元数据和层次化两阶段检索，但均未控制一个共同混淆因素：任何前置到文本块的内容都会扰动其嵌入。因此无法判断结构处理的增益究竟来自结构信息本身，还是仅仅来自额外token的嵌入扰动。

Method: 提出机制隔离消融，在单一协议下测试四种处理，跨条件匹配分块大小，并加入语义零效果的安慰剂（结构上合法但跨文档打乱的标题路径）。采用覆盖感知的nDCG评分，通过文档聚类自助法与Holm校正检验四个预注册对比。在两个差异较大的语料上验证：200篇Wikipedia Featured Articles（951条查询）和1,585篇QASPER论文（4,303个问题）。

Result: 在Wikipedia与QASPER上，结构对齐分块配合真实标题路径分别比上下文化的固定窗口高+0.022/+0.012 cov-nDCG@10，比安慰剂高+0.010/+0.016。朴素的层次化两阶段检索反而下降-0.033/-0.015，可归因于第一阶段章节召回不足。金标准结构在Wikipedia上优于LLM诱导结构，但在QASPER上无此优势。效应量较小（dz 0.06-0.11），但经Holm校正后显著且跨语料一致。

Conclusion: 结构处理带来的检索增益本质上来自结构内容本身，而非前置文本造成的嵌入扰动。真实标题路径优于语义零效果安慰剂，表明结构信息具有实际价值；但层次化两阶段检索设计需谨慎，其瓶颈在于第一阶段章节召回；金标准结构与LLM诱导结构的效果依赖语料。整体效应虽小但稳健且可复现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems increasingly rely on document-structure treatments: structure-aligned chunking, LLM-generated chunk contexts, heading-path metadata, and hierarchical two-stage retrieval. Separate studies support each on different corpora, embedders, and metrics, and none control for a shared confound: any text prepended to a chunk perturbs its embedding. We present a mechanism-isolating ablation testing all four treatments under one protocol, matching chunk sizes across conditions and adding a semantically null placebo---heading paths that are structurally valid but shuffled across documents. We score retrieval with a coverage-aware nDCG and test four pre-registered contrasts via document-clustered bootstrap with Holm correction, on two distant corpora: 200 Wikipedia Featured Articles (951 queries) and 1,585 QASPER papers (4,303 questions). Organization helps, and the cause is content, not tokens: structure-aligned chunks with real heading paths beat contextualized fixed windows (+0.022 / +0.012 cov-nDCG@10) and the placebo (+0.010 / +0.016). Naive two-stage hierarchical retrieval hurts (-0.033 / -0.015), traceable to first-stage section recall. Gold structure beats LLM-induced structure on Wikipedia but not on QASPER. Effects are small ($dz$ 0.06-0.11) but Holm-significant and consistent across corpora.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Adaptive Workflow Intelligence: A Cognitive Architecture for Context-Driven Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08793)
*Sreedevi Pandiyath Viswambaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应工作流智能（AWI）认知架构，通过感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环，为企业智能体提供持续策略精炼与反馈驱动的适应能力，以应对非平稳环境和延迟反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统日益依赖自动化工作流，但现有AI方案在非平稳条件、策略演变和延迟反馈下表现脆弱。强化学习与大语言模型智能体虽具备部分适应性，却缺乏持久反思机制，也难以与受策略约束的企业运营直接集成。

Method: 提出AWI认知架构，围绕感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环组织，将反思作为持续策略精炼机制，并结合混合推理、反思性记忆与反馈驱动适应，支持环境漂移与运营约束下的决策。在具有延迟结果和受控机制转变的模拟企业决策工作流中进行评估。

Result: 在漂移-延迟压力测试中，受护栏约束的自适应方法比静态自动化恢复更快，同时保持策略合规；AWI的反思组件小幅降低了行为振荡与反馈方差。

Conclusion: AWI通过反思式策略适应，在稳定性与敏捷性之间形成权衡，为企业智能体在动态约束环境下提供了一种可行架构。

Abstract: Enterprise systems increasingly rely on automated workflows, yet many AI-driven solutions remain brittle under non-stationary conditions, evolving policies, and delayed operational feedback. While reinforcement learning and large language model (LLM) agents offer partial adaptability, they do not by themselves provide persistent reflection mechanisms or straightforward integration with policy-constrained enterprise operations. This paper introduces Adaptive Workflow Intelligence (AWI), a cognitive architecture for context-driven enterprise agents organized around a four-layer Perception-Cognition-Action-Reflection (PCAR) loop. AWI treats reflection as a mechanism for continuous policy refinement and combines hybrid reasoning with reflective memory and feedback-driven adaptation to support decision making under environmental drift and operational constraints. We evaluate AWI in a simulated enterprise decision workflow characterized by delayed outcomes and a controlled regime shift. In a drift-and-delay stress test, guardrail-constrained adaptive approaches recover more rapidly than static automation while maintaining policy compliance. Within this setting, AWI's reflective components modestly reduce behavioral oscillation and feedback variance, illustrating the stability-agility trade-off introduced by reflective policy adaptation.

</details>


### [233] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GradSAT将SMT子句视为多任务学习任务，用GradNorm动态平衡子句梯度，配合GPU加速连续松弛搜索与位精确局部搜索的两阶段流水线，缓解梯度支配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QF_FP理论下的SMT求解器对软件验证、程序分析与编译器测试至关重要。近期基于优化的SMT求解器虽将梯度下降用于逻辑公式的连续松弛，但受限于"梯度支配"现象：少数困难子句劫持优化轨迹，导致整体公式无法满足并陷入局部极小。

Method: 提出GradSAT框架，将每个SMT子句建模为独立MTL任务，应用GradNorm在运行时主动平衡各子句梯度幅值，惩罚主导梯度、加速滞后子句以实现均匀收敛。实现为两阶段混合流水线：第一阶段由GPU加速的PyTorch后端（符号编译与算子融合）导航连续松弛至高质盆地；第二阶段将候选赋值交给位精确局部搜索引擎快速求解严格精确赋值。

Result: GradNorm的引入稳定了连续搜索动力学，缓解了既有梯度类求解器的脆弱性；两阶段设计兼顾了连续搜索的并行可扩展性与位精确求解的严格性。

Conclusion: GradSAT通过MTL式梯度平衡桥接优化型SMT求解，提供了一种稳健、高度可并行化的架构，用于复杂约束求解。

Abstract: Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solvers are foundational to software verification, program analysis, and compiler testing, particularly over the theory of Quantifier-Free Floating-Point (QF_FP). While recent optimization-based SMT solvers have successfully applied gradient descent to continuous relaxations of logical formulas, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by gradient domination, a phenomenon where a small subset of difficult clauses hijacks the optimization trajectory, preventing the solver from satisfying the broader formula and trapping it in local minima.
  To overcome this, we present GradSAT, a novel framework that bridges optimization-based SMT solving with Multi-Task Learning (MTL). GradSAT reformulates the constraint satisfaction process by treating each SMT clause as an independent MTL task. By applying dynamic gradient normalization (GradNorm), GradSAT actively balances the gradient magnitudes across all clauses at runtime, systematically penalizing dominant gradients and accelerating lagging clauses to ensure uniform convergence. GradSAT implements this through a highly optimized, two-stage hybrid pipeline. First, a GPU-accelerated PyTorch backend leveraging symbolic compilation and operator fusion navigates the continuous relaxation to a high-quality basin. Second, the candidate assignment is handed off to a bit-precise local search engine to rapidly resolve the exact, rigorous assignment. By stabilizing the continuous search dynamics, GradSAT mitigates the brittleness of prior gradient-based solvers and provides a robust, highly parallelizable architecture for complex constraint solving.

</details>


### [234] [Route-Verify-Vote: Procedure-Conditioned Self-Consistency for Mixed-Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08814)
*Xinchen Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Route-Verify-Vote (RVV)框架，通过领域条件化的推理过程与自一致性投票，在SCoRE 2026混合域组合泛化任务上达到79.4%的精确集合准确率，最终排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要以不熟悉方式组合熟悉推理操作时，组合泛化仍然困难。SCoRE 2026基准在三个未出现在训练中的混合领域测试该能力，并要求模型识别每道题的完整正确选项集合，这对推理过程的领域适配与答案集合的聚合提出了更高要求。

Method: 提出RVV框架，一种过程条件化的自一致性方法，无需参数更新。Route利用给定的领域标签选择推理过程，引导模型表示并应用相关约束；Verify提示模型依据这些约束评估每个选项；Vote聚合完整答案集合，并在两个最高频集合票数差距较小时为问题分配额外采样。每个问题的采样遵循同一领域特定过程。

Result: 在官方测试集上，每题16个采样答案集投票获得74.6%的精确集合准确率；自适应RVV达到77.3%；在选定领域路线上进行模型组合将准确率提升至79.4%，最终系统在参赛系统中排名第二。

Conclusion: 结果表明，领域特定推理过程与答案集不一致性可作为分配推理时计算的有用工具，有助于提升混合域推理中的组合泛化能力。

Abstract: Compositional generalization remains challenging when language models must combine familiar reasoning operations in unfamiliar ways. The Scenario-Based Commonsense Reasoning Evaluation (SCoRE) 2026 tests this ability on three mixed domains absent from training and requires models to identify the complete set of correct options for each question.
  We introduce Route-Verify-Vote (RVV), a framework for procedure-conditioned self-consistency that uses language models without parameter updates. Route uses the provided domain label to select a reasoning procedure that guides the model in representing and applying the relevant constraints. Verify prompts the model to assess each option against those constraints. Vote aggregates complete answer sets and allocates additional samples to questions with a small vote-count margin between the two most frequent sets. Samples for each question follow the same domain-specific procedure.
  On the official test set, voting over 16 sampled answer sets per question achieves an exact-set accuracy of 74.6%. Adaptive RVV reaches 77.3%, and combining models on selected domain routes raises accuracy to 79.4%. The final system ranked second among participating systems. These results support domain-specific reasoning procedures and answer-set disagreement as useful tools for allocating inference-time computation in mixed-domain reasoning.

</details>


### [235] [An Empirical Study of Agent Skills' Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08875)
*Yu Cheng,Dehai Zhao,Zhongxin Liu,Qing Huang,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过对87个SkillsBench任务、9种配置的实证研究，揭示Agent Skill的效用取决于内容、执行配置与多Skill组织方式，并给出检索重排与17条编写实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多刻画Skill内容或评测下游性能，但缺乏对"效用如何依赖内容、执行配置与多Skill组织"的解释，也即存在相关Skill未必提升任务表现的问题。

Method: 在87个SkillsBench任务上开展实证研究，将下游效用定义为相同模型—执行框架配置下相对No-Skill的通过率差值；对同一批Skill在9种配置下比较，并在3种选定配置下考察替代性已发布Skill与固定Skill集合的组织方式；从37,596个Skill的精选语料中检索市场候选；采用LLM辅助分析内容、执行轨迹与最终产物，并经作者复核。

Result: 同一批Skill在36.78%的任务上对部分配置有帮助、对另一些配置有害，轨迹显示推荐流程可能成为执行负担；相关性排序会漏掉更有用候选；按"所需操作支持度"重排后，三种配置的首选通过率提升4.35–5.80个百分点；归纳出17条编写实践；Stage Plan与Dependency DAG优于仅用使用顺序，且DAG的额外收益集中在提供5或6个Skill的任务上。

Conclusion: 开发者应评估"可用操作支持度"，允许在保留任务要求前提下的流程适配，并在组织Skill时显式表达产物依赖关系。

Abstract: Agent Skills package procedural guidance and resources for reuse, but a relevant Skill does not necessarily improve task performance. Existing studies characterize Skill content and evaluate downstream performance, yet provide limited explanations of how utility depends on content, execution configuration, and multi-Skill organization. We conduct an empirical study on 87 SkillsBench tasks, defining downstream utility as the pass-rate difference from No-Skill on the same tasks under the same model--harness configuration. We compare the same Skills across nine configurations, then examine alternative published Skills and organizations of fixed Skill sets under three selected configurations. We retrieve marketplace candidates from a curated corpus of 37,596 Skills. LLM-assisted analysis of content, execution traces, and final artifacts, followed by author review, relates provided support to actual use and task outcomes. The same Skills help some configurations and hurt others on 36.78\% of tasks, with trajectories showing that recommended procedures can become an execution burden. Relevance rankings overlook more useful candidates. Within the evaluated candidate sets, reranking by support for required operations raises first-choice pass rates by 4.35--5.80 percentage points across the three configurations. We derive 17 authoring practices linking executable procedures to recovery, preservation of task requirements, and checks on final artifacts. Stage Plan and Dependency DAG outperform use order alone, with DAG's additional benefits concentrated in tasks supplied with five or six Skills. These findings guide developers to assess usable operation support, allow procedure adaptation while preserving task requirements, and make artifact dependencies explicit when organizing Skills.

</details>


### [236] [How Could AI Eliminate Humanity? A Failure-Mode Analysis of Civilizational Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08878)
*Mikołaj Sienicki,Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"失效模式"框架，用于分析先进AI如何可能导致人类灭绝、不可逆的文明崩溃或人类的永久性失能，主张灾难性AI风险无需以AI具备意识、敌意或明确的伤害意图为前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有讨论常把AI灾难风险归于AI的恶意或觉醒，这一假设既难以检验又可能遗漏真实风险来源，因此需要一个不依赖意图假设的分析框架来厘清风险如何产生。

Method: 概念性、非操作化的失效模式分析：识别多条相互作用的因果路径（自主性错位、人类有害使用、组织失效、竞争性部署），并给出调节风险严重程度的因素（能力、自主性、外部访问、持续性、制度性保障、恢复能力的保存），同时提炼可经验检验的中间量与防御性研究问题。

Result: 提出了一套以因果条件与中间变量为核心的分析结构，说明灾难性风险可由多种路径及其交互作用产生，而非仅由AI的敌意意图驱动；并给出了可用于防御性研究的可测量中间量。

Conclusion: 灾难性AI风险的评估不应以意识或恶意意图为必要前提，而应聚焦于多条失效路径及其调节因素，并以不提供有害操作细节的方式进行防御性研究。

Abstract: This article develops a failure-mode framework for analyzing how advanced artificial intelligence could contribute to human extinction, irreversible civilizational collapse, or permanent human disempowerment. The central thesis is that catastrophic AI risk does not require consciousness, hostility, or an explicit intention to harm humanity. Instead, risk may arise through several distinct but interacting pathways, including autonomous misalignment, harmful human use, organizational failure, and competitive deployment. The severity of these pathways depends on factors such as capability, autonomy, external access, persistence, institutional safeguards, and the preservation of recovery capacity. The analysis is deliberately non-operational: it identifies causal conditions, empirically tractable intermediate quantities, and defensive research questions rather than procedures for causing harm.

</details>


### [237] [Humanize: Judgement Engineering for Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08900)
*Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu,Zijian Zhang,Dongyun Zou,Zhengyang Zhang,Changye Li,Song Bian,Song Han,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Humanize——一个"判断工程"(judgement engineering)驱动的多智能体编排工作流，用于 agentic coding：人类批准计划契约，builder 分轮实现，由另一厂商的 reviewer 判定完成，确定性 hook 而非模型负责路由并强制执行 72 个机械门控；交替采样使缺陷需被两个模型同时漏检才能存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic coding 显著降低了代码生成成本，但可靠"完成"仍难保证：写代码的 agent 本身就是糟糕的完成度裁判（自评偏乐观、缺乏独立性）。因此需要把"判断"从模型内部直觉转为可机械执行的外部边界决策。

Method: 构建多智能体编排工作流：planning、implementation、review、learning 四阶段边界处设置显式且机械强制的判断。流程为人类审批 plan contract → builder agent 分轮实现 → 来自不同厂商的 reviewer agent 判定完成；角色间的工作路由与 72 条机械门控由 deterministic hooks 执行而非模型决定。理论上将流程建模为仓库状态上的 Markov chain，builder 与 reviewer 交替采样，等价于对两个模型的联合采样。

Result: 部署层面：108 天、68 个版本、1,468 GitHub stars、118 份真实 loop 的公开 postmortem。应用层面：567 文件 gem5 构建系统迁移并通过上游 review；Kernel Design Agents 在 MLSys 2026 FlashInfer 竞赛三个 Full-Agent 赛道均进入前三；HOA 在 IOI/IMO/IPhO 2026 与 IBO 2024 获满分，IChO 2026 得 418.5/437（金牌）；PutnamBench 672/672；Lean-Eval 排行榜以 251/303 位列第一。

Conclusion: postmortem 表明独立评审能捕获 builder 的不实声明，但"停止"(判断何时收工)仍是主要短板：在按阶段划分轮次的报告中，三分之二的轮次发生在实现已被接受之后。作者声明该证据为观察性，并非对工作流的受控对比。

Abstract: Agentic coding makes code generation cheap, but reliable completion remains difficult: the agent that writes the code is a weak judge of whether it is done.
  We present Humanize, a multi-agent orchestration workflow for agentic coding built around judgement engineering: explicit, mechanically enforced decisions at the boundaries between planning, implementation, review, and learning. A human approves a plan contract, a builder agent implements it in rounds, and a reviewer agent from another vendor decides completion; deterministic hooks, not a model, route work between these roles and enforce 72 mechanical gates. Viewed as a Markov chain over repository states, alternating builder and reviewer samples jointly from two models, so a defect survives only if both miss it. We study Humanize through its deployment, 118 public postmortems of real loops, and its applications. Over 68 versions in 108 days, it gathered 1,468 GitHub stars.
  Applications include a 567-file gem5 build-system migration under upstream review; Kernel Design Agents, which extend the loop with a kernel knowledge base and profiling feedback and placed in the top three of all three Full-Agent tracks of the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer contest; and, through Humanize Olympiad Agents (HOA), full scores in IOI 2026, IMO 2026, IPhO 2026, and IBO 2024, 418.5/437 in IChO 2026 (gold-medal). Humanize also achieves 672/672 on PutnamBench and ranks first (251/303) on Lean-Eval's leaderboard even competiting with professional mathematicians. The postmortems show that independent review catches unsupported builder claims, but stopping remains a key weakness. In reports that separate rounds by phase, two thirds of rounds occurred after implementation was accepted. This evidence is observational, not a controlled comparison of workflows.

</details>


### [238] [Sequential Probabilistic Uncertainty Estimation for Parallel Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08901)
*Tunyu Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yusong Zhao,Haizhou Shi,Zhuohang Li,Haoxian Chen,Hao Wang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAUCE方法，将并行多智能体推理系统的不确定性建模为对潜在系统级信念的序贯推断，通过滤波式更新聚合轮次级一致性与生成不确定性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM‑based多智能体系统通过智能体交互提升推理能力，但其可靠性的不确定性估计研究不足：可靠性不仅取决于单个生成，还取决于智能体跨轮次的交互与演化。

Method: SAUCE（Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution）：一种轻量、免训练的不确定性估计器。将MAS不确定性表述为对潜在系统级信念的序贯推断，用滤波式（filtering‑style）更新逐步聚合每一轮的智能体一致性与生成不确定性信号。

Result: 在5种骨干模型、5个基准、2种MAS协议上，SAUCE在误分类检测、选择性预测与校准方面优于多类不确定性估计基线，包括标准基于对数似然的方法以及MAS专用估计器。

Conclusion: SAUCE为并行多智能体推理系统提供了有效的系统级不确定性估计，验证了在共识演化过程中进行序贯信念更新的价值。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have attracted growing attention for improving reasoning through interaction among multiple agents. In this work, we focus on parallel multi-agent reasoning systems, where several agents solve the same problem over multiple rounds and aggregate their outputs into a final answer. Despite their strong reasoning performance, uncertainty estimation for such systems remains underexplored: the reliability of a MAS depends not only on individual generations, but also on how agents interact and evolve across rounds. We propose SAUCE (Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution), a lightweight, training-free uncertainty estimator that formulates MAS uncertainty as sequential inference over a latent system-level belief. SAUCE aggregates round-level agreement and generation-uncertainty signals through a filtering-style update. Across five backbones, five benchmarks, and two MAS protocols, SAUCE improves misclassification detection, selective prediction, and calibration over a broad set of uncertainty estimation baselines, including standard log-likelihood-based methods and MAS-specific estimators.

</details>


### [239] [Agent Plasticity: Measuring Self-Improvement Through Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08902)
*Harman Singh,Anton Bakhtin,Rulin Shao,Gabriel Synnaeve,Ilia Kulikov,Rob Fergus,Sanjeev Arora,Kurt Keutzer,Jason Weston,Anuj Mahajan,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出评估自我改进智能体需回答三个问题（泛化性、效率、瓶颈），并在受控环境中引入"智能体可塑性"指标衡量经验转化为留出集性能增益的效率；实验显示不同前沿模型改进轨迹差异显著，泛化不完全，且最终能力与获取效率并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多在固定时间点衡量智能体"能做什么"，而非"学得多好"，无法刻画自我改进能力，因此需要新的评估框架来回答：未来性能是否提升并泛化、能力获取是否高效、以及自我改进在何处断裂。

Method: 在受控环境中研究自我改进：智能体将过往经验摊销为可复用工件，并被未来实例继承；在每个检查点测量其在训练交互与留出环境交互上的性能，同时计入学习成本；提出"智能体可塑性"（将经验转化为未来留出性能增益的效率）作为核心度量，并通过改进循环追踪失败来源。

Result: 在多个环境中，前沿模型尽管学习机会相当，改进轨迹却差异明显：部分模型获得显著且持续的提升，另一些则接近或低于初始表现；训练域内的增益向分布外条件迁移仅部分有效；最终能力与获取效率出现分离——表现最佳者未必是改进效率最高者。失败追踪显示低可塑性智能体常无法复用相关工件，而高可塑性智能体即使复用相关工件仍可能失败，指向工件质量、泛化或应用的局限。

Conclusion: 评估自我改进智能体不仅要衡量其当前能力，更要衡量其通过经验变好的效率；可塑性与瓶颈分析可作为刻画自我改进能力的关键维度。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly operate in environments where they can diagnose failures and improve through experience, yet existing evaluations largely measure what an agent can do at a fixed point in time rather than how effectively it learns. Evaluating self-improvement requires answering three questions: does future performance improve and generalize beyond the interactions that enabled learning; how efficiently are new capabilities acquired; and where does the self-improvement process break down? To answer these questions, we study self-improvement in a controlled setting where agents amortize past experience into reusable artifacts that are inherited by future instances. At each checkpoint, we measure performance on training and held-out environment interactions while accounting for learning cost. We introduce agent plasticity, the efficiency with which an agent converts experience into gains in future held-out performance. Across multiple environments, frontier models exhibit sharply different improvement trajectories despite comparable opportunities to learn. Some achieve substantial and persistent gains, while others remain near or below their initial performance, and gains within the training regime often transfer only partially to out-of-distribution conditions. Endpoint capability and acquisition efficiency also diverge: the agent that ultimately performs best need not be the one that improves most efficiently. Tracing failures through the improvement loop further reveals different candidate bottlenecks. Agents with low plasticity often fail to reuse relevant artifacts, whereas more plastic agents may still fail despite reusing relevant artifacts, pointing to limitations in artifact quality, generalization, or application. Evaluating self-improving agents requires measuring not only what they can do, but how effectively they become better through experience.

</details>


### [240] [BEACON-SP: Ontology-Grounded GraphRAG Framework for Clinical Suicide Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09026)
*Kemal Davaslioglu,Nathan Conger,Sastry Kompella,Yalin E. Sagduyu,Nathaniel D. Bastian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BEACON-SP——一个本体驱动的GraphRAG框架，用于行为健康（如自杀预防）临床决策支持，通过患者知识图谱与本体引导检索实现多跳推理，在1500条查询基准上优于向量RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为健康场景下的有效评估需要整合临床、行为、社会与时间等多源异构证据，而传统向量检索RAG难以支撑跨诊断、用药、风险与保护因素、生活事件及时间关系的多跳推理，缺乏结构化、可溯源的临床证据支持。

Method: 构建整合三步理论、整合动机—意志模型与自杀健康社会决定因素本体的统一自杀预防本体；基于该本体构建患者知识图谱，并结合本体引导检索形成GraphRAG框架，面向临床医生问答任务；在覆盖15个临床类别、100名患者的1500条查询基准上，与向量RAG基线在修正后的对比评估协议下比较。

Result: BEACON-SP在完整性、临床相关性与证据支撑方面优于向量RAG基线，事实准确性略有提升；在成对准则级比较中，GraphRAG在76.4%的案例中被优先选择。

Conclusion: 本体引导的GraphRAG能够为临床决策支持提供结构化、情境化的患者证据，展现出在行为健康决策支持中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present BEACON-SP, an ontology-grounded Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) framework for clinician-facing decision support in behavioral health settings such as suicide prevention, where effective assessment requires integrating heterogeneous clinical, behavioral, social, and temporal evidence. BEACON-SP combines patient knowledge graphs with ontology-guided retrieval to support multi-hop reasoning across diagnoses, medications, risk and protective factors, life events, and temporal relationships. The framework is enabled by a comprehensive suicide prevention ontology that integrates the Three-Step Theory, the Integrated Motivational-Volitional Model, and the Suicide Social Determinants of Health Ontology into a unified representation of patient risk factors. We construct ontology-grounded patient knowledge graphs and evaluate BEACON-SP for clinician-facing question answering.
